Ids are read case-insensitively and as `[\w-]+`, as the LIKE pre-filter, `imageIsReferenced`, Express routing and the rich-text sanitiser read them (lock: the upper-case and `_` arm). **The two erasure deletes of older certificates' share images (`lib/account-reaper.js`, the event DELETE) do not ask the manifest, on purpose:** they erase the deleted person's or event's own artwork, and erasure wins as it does for frozen boards: no avatar may name such an image (the avatar rule above), but challenge rich text (and its versions) can, and loses it then. **`GET /api/images/:id` is `private, max-age=86400` with the id as a strong ETag** (304 on a match, 404 once the row is gone): a deleted picture must not outlive its row in a shared cache or for a year in a browser (copies fetched under the old `public, immutable` header before 9.2.18 can still last a year; nothing reaches them), and a per-view revalidation would spend a venue NAT's shared images bucket (lock: `tests/image-sweep.spec.js`). **Deleting a person strikes their avatar from every frozen team board** (`lib/account-reaper.js` `_scrubBoardAvatars`, in the cascade's transaction, to the preset `default1`: the board row's `avatar` is text on the wire) and reclaims each one, so for the avatar erasure wins over a board (the row keeps their nickname and userId: *Known open gaps*); a REPLACED avatar a board names is kept (lock: `tests/image-sweep.spec.js`).
- **`deleteUserCascade` removes rows about the user; rows naming them as actor (`changed_by`) survive** (README GDPR procedure).

#### Logs pane and perf spans
- **Global sort only under a narrowing filter** (`hasNarrowingFilter`; `date`, `anchor` excluded), row-native columns, capped by `LOG_SORT_MAX_WINDOW`, one walk at a time; fallbacks report `sort_unavailable`. The pager keys on `sorted_globally`, `total_exact` (not `!truncated`: a `since` stop is exact) and `truncated_reason`.
- **`anchor_ts` (the pager's pin) is the newest match in WALK order (`headTs`), never from the sorted page,** else later pages drift or repeat.
- **The Audit log stream (`stream=app`) pages like the audit trail (operator ruling, 17 Nov 2026), decided by ONE predicate (`pagesLikeTrail`):** only the first read (no anchor or cursor, offset 0) is counted, capped at the trail's `AUDIT_COUNT_MAX` (`LOG_COUNT_MAX`, `count_cap`); a later read answers `total: null` and stops at its page plus one; `next_cursor` is a byte position `[file, start]` below the page's last row (`_readLinesReverse` yields each line's `start`, and reads only below `end`), with its file's inode, whose file must be one of the stream's own readdir list, never a path built from the request, found by that inode so a size rotation's rename is followed (400 `logs_cursor_invalid`; with an anchor, `logs_position_conflict`); a cursor read is never ranked globally (a sort reorders that page, `sort_unavailable: cursor`); a walk `LOG_SCAN_BUDGET` cuts answers `searched_to` and a cursor below the last line examined. The Request log keeps a count on every read and takes no cursor (the hot file, polled). The Audit log's page-1 refresh is a counted first read (up to 10 001 lines per tick per open pane). The client's refresh holds off while a load the operator asked for is in flight (`S._logsMove`): the pager moves only when an answer lands, so a refresh fired meanwhile would supersede the move. Locks: `tests/logs-audit-paging.api.spec.js`, `tests/logs-pager-ui.spec.js`.
- **Instrumented spans taking a body use `_perfSplit`;** slow means `serverMs`, a missing body end is null not zero, and only `clientBodyMs - loopBlockedMs` is excused.

### Import, export and backups

#### Export drift guards
- **Every `users`, `tasks` and `competitions` column is a decision: exported, or exempted with its reason.** An unclassified column fails. Locks: `tests/functional-security-a.api.spec.js` (users), `tests/tasks-drift.spec.js`, `tests/competitions-drift.spec.js`.
- **Delete a column's entry when you drop the column:** each guard also fails on an `EXPORTED` or `EXCLUDED` entry naming a missing column.
- **A participant-authored users field goes in `PROFILE_IN_CSV` and `exportParticipantsCSV`.** The users guard round-trips every exported column through JSON (`role` has its own test) and checks every `PROFILE_IN_CSV` field in the CSV. The `competition_users`/`hint_reveals` guards check one named column each and miss new ones.
- **Never exported:** credentials and capabilities (`password_hash`, `password_set_at`, all verify/reset/login-code columns, `cxone_api_key`, `self_enroll_token`, `join_code`); `trusted_network_ips` (the file carries the start time, so an import would re-arm the bypass); identity bindings (`linkedin_sub`, `tasks.created_by_id`, left NULL on import); `id`, `updated_at` (a foreign value seeds an If-Match for edits never made here); navigation state; `tasks.locked`; `login_method` (no password travels, so an imported account starts at its role's default).
- **Event-kind columns, registration policy, the legacy LinkedIn-signup column, event `attempt_cap` and the late-entry columns stay excluded: there is no competitions import endpoint.** Older settings are exported, including `cxone_disabled` (lost, the target creates CxOne identities) and `staff_compete` (lost, staff drop off the board).
- **Exported because losing them breaks something:** `users.profile_policy` (else the completion gate hits every migrated walk-up), certificate names, absence `login_time`/`assigned_at`, `tasks.attempt_cap`, `tasks.answer_sealed`.

#### Import rules
- **Without `resolutions` an import is a dry run** that returns conflicts; an unresolved conflict defaults to `keep`.
- **Malformed file = 400 naming the row; unimportable row = skip.** A wrong-typed row or field (`server.js` `_importRowShapeError`, `_IMPORT_FIELD_SHAPES`) refuses the batch even in a dry run, where a skipped row would desync the conflict indices the UI sends back. A well-typed but unusable value (bad enum, blank title, unanswerable choice row) is skipped.
- **Shapes refuse only what cannot be bound.** better-sqlite3 spreads an array as positional binds and throws on objects. `flag` takes any scalar, normalised to 0/1 at the write (the platform's own export emits legacy `active: 99`). Shape-check every bound value, including the overwrite branch and the `_rehydrate*` helpers.
- **Every clamp or drop is counted and shown to the operator** by `_importDropNote` (`public/js/features/export-import.js`). A new drop needs a counter there; a silent drop is a defect.
- **A role unknown to this installation is dropped** (built-in and existing custom roles are honoured; `roleDefinition`): an insert falls back to `participant`, an overwrite keeps the existing role (no CHECK constraint; a file must not demote an admin). The `staff` tag the import re-adds and `role_grants` follow the role's class (`isGlobalClassRole`), as the role route does. An unusable timezone is cleared; the completion gate recovers it. An avatar given as a `/api/images/` link is stored only when it names the overwritten account's own current picture (the avatar rule of every door: an insert may name none); a link to no stored picture or to another account's is not stored (an overwrite keeps the current avatar, an insert gets `default1`) and is counted (`avatar_links_dropped`). Avatars an overwrite replaced are reclaimed once, after the last row of the whole import (`_replacedAvatars`, truncated with a rolled-back chunk) (lock: `tests/import-field-shapes.spec.js`).
- **A sealed task row is refused:** import writes no `created_by_id`, so it would land authorless. A `hint_withheld` row keeps the stored hint on overwrite and lands hintless on create. Choice rows use `lib/choices.js` `storedChoiceRowError`; ids are kept verbatim, as re-minting them severs `correct_answer`.
- **The competitor lock holds at every import door** (`server.js` `_rehydrate*`, challenge import). Rows enrolling anyone else into an event the importer competes in, and absence rows on it (and the importer's own on any compete event), skip as `actor_competes`; the importer's own enrolment into a compete event gets `login_time` NULL (a file-supplied anchor is a chosen placing). A group bound to such an event is dropped (`group_actor_competes_dropped`); a task it holds is not overwritten (`competitor_skipped`); nor is a rival's account (`competitorRivalDenied`: the overwrite writes `blocked`, `verified`, `approved`, role and tags), skipped whole as `rival_skipped`.
- **A non-superadmin importer (a custom role holding `data.import`) is held to the escalation rule:** overwriting an account that is a privileged target for them (`privilegedTargetFor`), or a row giving a role beyond their own (`roleAssignDenied`), is skipped whole and counted (`privileged_skipped`), read at the write (lock: `tests/import-field-shapes.spec.js`, with a superadmin control).
- **The dry run's `conflicts` are a snapshot from before the awaits (ZIP validation, the challenge import's rich-text pass, the commit-time park).** Every check guarding a write reads the row after the last await: lock (the attachment pre-flight too), existence, freeze, fit, seal, competitor, rival, and an insert's email and nickname. A value carried from the snapshot overrules whatever changed meanwhile (lock: `tests/import-inflight-state.api.spec.js`). An overwrite skips a row before counting it or storing its avatar.
- **Participant import commits in 100-row chunks; a failure keeps the earlier chunks and returns `partial:`,** which the client shows, then reloads. Every import path checks `r.ok` and drops its overlay on failure (participant paths in `finally`). Challenge import is one transaction.
- **The challenge import sanitises its rich text (`sanitiseRichText`, pure CPU) in a pass that yields every `IMPORT_RICH_TEXT_SLICE_MS`, ahead of the commit-time re-check; storing the embedded images (`extractAndStoreImages`, a write) and the clamps stay in the transaction,** so a failed, refused or skipped row stores no image (an orphan waits a week for the image sweep) and `fields_truncated`/`images_dropped` count only rows written (lock: `tests/import-rich-text-prepass.api.spec.js`).
- **Re-check primary and secondary email inside the write transaction, after the last await:** UNIQUE indexes miss the cross-slot case.
- **Attachments:** a failed ZIP validation unlinks every validated temp file, not just the failing (last) one. After commit, promoted ZIPs not in `insertedFinalIds` (by uuid) are unlinked. Do not copy the skip predicate into pre-flight.
- **Import UI choices never reach unseen rows.** Participant select-all and its header checkbox use `_importMatchingIndices()` (search-filtered, shared with the renderer). Conflict choices live in `overlay._res` (read by `executeConflictImport`, a rendered radio overriding; set in full by `setAllConflictResolutions`), never the DOM with a `keep` fallback, which imports off-screen rows as keep.

#### CSV writers and formula neutralisation
- **`lib/csv.js` and `public/js/ui/csv.js` mirror each other:** both always quote, double inner quotes, use LF and a UTF-8 BOM, and share `FORMULA_TRIGGERS`. `neutraliseFormula` adds an apostrophe prefix (after any leading whitespace/NBSP) before quoting. Quoting is not neutralisation. (lock: `tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js`)
- **Client `csvCell` neutralises by default; exempt a column by its header label through `exemptColumns`, with the reason at the call site.** Only `Phone` in the participants export is exempt, and only for a plain number shape (`toCsv` `PLAIN_NUMBER_SHAPE`); any other value in an exempt column is neutralised. The server writer has no exemption path.
- **Server CSV responses use `csvBuffer`, an explicit `res.type("text/csv; charset=utf-8")`, `Content-Disposition: attachment` and `_slugForDownload`.** `res.send(string)` goes out as text/html, allowing stored XSS through names.
- **Headers use the UI labels, not schema names, and never vary by event:** they are a contract with the reader.
- **Rankings CSV** (`public/js/features/rankings-admin.js`): 'Time on Platform' and its ms column always sit beside 'Time to Solve', blank when `presenceMs` is null; never `|| 0`, and guard the formatted cell (`fmtSolveTime(null)` is `-`). `exportRankingsCsv` reads the rows each rendered board registered in `_RK_EXPORTS` (`_rkRowsInView`, or `renderGroupBoardTable` for the group board; after the search box and heading filters), never `S._rkCache`, which can hold the previous event. The group file says 'Avg Score' and '(team total)'. Keep the static Export CSV button in `public/index.html`: the dynamic one appears only without `#admin-rankings-body`.
- **People's Export CSV writes `participantsInView()` (`features/participants-admin.js`), the table's own pipeline, never a filter of its own** (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026): every page the table lists, in its order, after the status chips, the open event's people (or everyone, with Search everyone ticked), the search box and the heading filters; with nobody matching it saves nothing and toasts the empty row's reason (both read `participantsNarrowing()`). Those filters outlive leaving the page, so the palette's Export people (CSV) opens People and exports once its read has drawn the table (`_exportPeopleOnTheirPage`): never export from a page that does not show them (lock: `tests/people-csv-export.spec.js`).
- **Competition-activity CSV** (`routes/export-import.js`): rows are the enrolment minus `staff` (unless staff compete), never the scoring helpers. It covers every assigned challenge, hidden ones too. Solved means a solve exists; a qualifying question's Points is 0, as it scores (the full export's `challenges.csv` too). Only Navigated Away excludes impersonated activity. Refused while `upcoming`. `ensureTaskNumbers` (a write) runs after `competitionDenied` and before the read snapshot. Grouped queries are joined in JS.
- **`GET .../free-text-responses` adds one column per `any` challenge to the focused participants CSV** from the latest `is_correct = 1` row (wrong rows survive a text-to-any switch). It has no `upcoming` gate: rosters are exported before the event opens.

#### Full event and person exports

- **Builders in `lib/full-export.js`; routes in `routes/export-import.js`** (`GET /api/admin/competitions/:id/export/full`, `GET /api/admin/participants/:id/export/full`). Read in one BEGIN…COMMIT snapshot; rankings come from `buildAdminLeaderboard` / `buildGroupLeaderboard` with `{fresh:true}`, per session in per-slot mode, empty for an unranked kind.
- **Format is a ZIP of CSVs, written by `lib/zip-writer.js` (pure, deflate via async zlib, no ZIP64, refuses traversal names).** `xlsx` is refused 400 `export_format_unsupported`, never answered with a ZIP. No new dependency (a lockfile change forces a CI image rebuild).
- **Participant set** is wider than the activity CSV's: everyone enrolled plus anyone with a submission or hint, minus `staff` unless staff compete; `benchmark` kept and tagged; applied to every per-person file but the rankings (the board builders' own) and certificates.csv, which lists every certificate issued in the event whoever holds it now (an issued certificate is a fact).
- **Sealed answers: `answerVisibleFor(req)`**, an adapter over `lib/answer-seal.js` `submissionAnswerProjection(req).visible` (the admin Submissions rule; never re-derive it) (author by `actingAdminId`, or a superadmin reveal not narrowed away by the competitor check). No file carries an answer key, badge token (a revocation records none, and `userEventDetailsSql` drops an older `badge_revoked` row's), credential code, join token or invitation id; the person profile is an ALLOW-LIST (`PERSON_PROFILE_FIELDS`), never `SELECT *`.
- **Gates.** Event export: 404, competitor lock (the file holds rivals' answers), scope, then `ensureTaskNumbers` (writes). Person export: `participantDenied` first (scope before existence), and no competitor lock: a competing admin may export a rival's file (operator ruling, 24 Nov 2026: a read, as Export Activity is, and a compete event holds sealed challenges only, so the answers stay withheld; lock: the sweep #233 test in `tests/event-full-export.api.spec.js`). For a scoped actor every section is narrowed to their events; History rows pass `userEventCompetitionScopeSql` alone, the clause the History dialog uses (see *Event-admin scope*); add no JS-side filter. After the deflate (the ZIP's one await) both re-ask the admin (`adminAuthzDenied` `{read:true}`), the scope read fresh (for the person, every event of the scope the file was built under) and, for the event, the competitor lock, then record and send (lock: `tests/full-export-inflight-authz.api.spec.js`).
- Every cell goes through `lib/csv.js`, so a `-5` points delta reads `'-5`. Each download emits orphaned `data_exported` (`competition-full` / `person-full`) with `reqContext`. Locks: `tests/event-full-export.api.spec.js` (decoy event, sealed-author control, formula cell, scoped person export by value); `tests/event-full-export-sets.api.spec.js` (the participant set by value: a removed participant kept by an answer or a hint alone, staff out where they do not compete with a before-the-tag control and kept where they do; a certificate still listed after its holder is tagged staff; boards per session, late board, no capability in any file).

#### Backups
- **`scripts/backup.sh` and `scripts/purge.sh` refuse an unset or empty `DATA_DIR`** (`${DATA_DIR:-}` under `set -u`) and resolve it to an absolute path, as RESTORE.txt bakes it into `rm` and `rsync --delete`. sudo and cron drop it: pass it after `sudo` and in the crontab line.
- **backup.sh refuses a missing or empty `hackathon.db` before creating anything, verifies the snapshot and writes `BACKUP_COMPLETE` last.** Never restore a directory without it. It refuses a destination holding `hackathon.db` or `BACKUP_METADATA.txt` unless `--reuse` is the second argument; that, not `--delete` on its rsyncs, is the chosen fix for stale files.
- **`BACKUP_COMPLETE` also vouches for every attachment file the snapshot names.** One missing from the backup forces a new snapshot (up to `BACKUP_MAX_ATTEMPTS`, then exit 1) unless the live volume lost it too: that is counted, not refused (operator ruling, 21 Oct 2026). A file the backup holds is still stat'ed live (a `--reuse` directory keeps earlier runs' copies): lost live while its row remains, it is counted and marked `this backup holds a copy`, and stays in `attachments_verified`. Never reorder copy and snapshot (lock: `tests/backup-attachment-reconcile.api.spec.js`).
- **Restore per RESTORE.txt: stop the app, delete `hackathon.db-wal`/`-shm`, `rsync -a --delete`, the email choice, `chown -R` to the recorded owner.** A leftover WAL is replayed over the snapshot, and `integrity_check` still reports `ok`.
- **A restore's step 4 is the email choice: `scripts/restore-mail.sh mark-sent|resend`, with the app stopped, between the rsync and the chown** (operator ruling, 17 Nov 2026). It refuses a `-wal`/`-shm` beside the database (a running server); `mark-sent` writes `settings.restore_mail_floor_at` (forward only, one instant shared with its audit row), marks every unsent start reminder already due as sent (`email_reminders`; the dispatcher also holds in maintenance, so without mark-sent they go out once it is lifted, those whose start has passed settled unsent) and writes a `settings_events` `updated` row by `system`; `resend` writes only the row. A floor more than a day ahead is ignored by the workers (logged once): it would stop all automatic mail. The floor is compared with the event's END, so after a mark-sent restore an event that ended before it gets no automatic certificate even for a late entrant who joins later, nor a team delta or 48-hour reminder (stated in README; Send now and the holder's own Get my certificate remain). **Never write `certificate_dispatch_floor_at` from a script:** `db.js` reads its absence as a database from 8.14 (the approval-gate and late-entry floors). Locks: `tests/restore-mail-script.spec.js`, the data-dir-required SOURCE and real-tools arms, the workers' arms in `tests/certificate-dispatch.spec.js` and `tests/event-team-reports.spec.js`.
- **`scripts/migrate.sh` (`pack`, `unpack`, `check`) moves an installation on top of backup.sh.** `unpack` only fills an empty `DATA_DIR`, deletes nothing and requires the checksum and `BACKUP_COMPLETE`; as root it chooses the owner, in order: `MIGRATE_OWNER` (refused before writing when malformed or naming no user or group here), a prepared `DATA_DIR`'s owner (not root), the backup's recorded `data_dir_owner`, else `1000:1000`, and prints which (sweep #145); never give it a replace mode (locks: `tests/migrate-script.spec.js`, refusals and the owner ladder; `tests/migrate-round-trip.spec.js`, a real pack → move → unpack, the 0600 archive, `lost+found`, `check`). Run as root, tar keeps the modes the archive recorded, so the restored files are not made owner-only; only the archive and its checksum are.
- **purge.sh never pipes paths to `xargs`** (`find ... -exec rm -f -- {} +`; size mode `rm -f -- "$pathv"`). `--max-mb` never deletes today's `app-`/`access-` logs, matching the UTC date against the file name only (a `DATA_DIR` path containing it would protect everything). What a backup holds: see *Architecture*.
- **Every sqlite3 call in the operator scripts (backup.sh, purge.sh, restore-mail.sh) passes `-init /dev/null -batch -noheader -list`:** sqlite3 reads `~/.sqliterc` (root's under sudo) even when handed SQL, which can reshape a count. purge.sh reads counts through `_count_rows`, which exits 1 on a failure or a non-number; never `|| echo 0` there (locks: `tests/data-dir-required.spec.js`; for backup.sh the S arm of `tests/backup-attachment-reconcile.api.spec.js`).

### Analytics

#### Events and their recording
- **Never add an `opened`/`viewed` type to `challenge_events`.** `unique_attempters` and `unique_participants` are `COUNT(DISTINCT ce.user_id)` with no `event_type` predicate, and `unique_attempters` is the solve-rate denominator, so a view type inflates both counts and silently lowers every solve rate; add an attempt-type allow-list first. Where: `server.js` `/api/admin/analytics/challenges`.
- **Impersonated events never count.** One `ce.impersonated=0` in the shared `whereSql` covers every query and event type; the flag is captured at queue time (`recordChallengeEvent` 7th arg). A lock must submit a wrong answer first and assert via the endpoint after exiting impersonation, or leaked `incorrect` rows pass unseen.
- **`navigated_away` means bounced, not left.** The route answers 200 `skipped:'already_solved'` once the user has a correct submission for that challenge in that competition; never 4xx, which would feed scanner reputation. The 2 s dedup is scoped by `impersonated=?` so an impersonated row cannot swallow the real one. Where: `routes/participant.js`.
- **Re-arm on tab return.** `rearmOpenTask` (called from the `visibilitychange` handler in `core/main.js`) restores `S._openedTaskId` from `S._navAwayResumeId`, only while `task-detail-page` is the active page; without it one tab switch silences that challenge for good.

#### Endpoint
- **Defaults exclude `staff` participants in every scope (`benchmark` stays in) and, in global scope only, events tagged `DRY_RUN_TAG`.**
- **Event admins asking platform-wide get 400 `event_admin_global_scope`**, never a silently narrowed answer.
- **Response caching: see *In-process caches and their invalidation*.** The wire sort (total, title, `task_id`) is total and deliberately not parameterised; the client re-sorts.

#### Pane (`public/js/features/analytics-admin.js`)
- **No query until a scope is chosen.** State opens `scope: null` and renders `_renderAnalyticsPrompt`, as a default looks like a choice; `_analyticsAdoptFocusedScope` adopts the open workspace's event/session before the single tab-entry fetch.
- **Inside a workspace Analytics is that event only** (owner's decision, 27 Nov 2026): the scope card (`#analytics-ws-scope`) names the event, with no Global/Event switch and no event picker, and offers Whole event or one of its sessions where it has any (`analytics-ws-set-timeslot`); the sidebar's Analytics is the cross-event page and keeps the switch (lock: `tests/analytics-workspace-scope.spec.js`).
- **`S._analyticsFetchSeq` is re-checked after fetch, after parse and on error.**
- **Competition/task lists are re-read on every tab entry, not cached**: nothing would invalidate them.
- **Export CSV is client-side (no endpoint) and filters only through `_anRowsInView`, the table renderer's own filter** (the search box and heading filters): it writes every filtered row, not just the page, so the file matches the screen; never give it a second filter path (CSV quoting and neutralisation: see *Import, export and backups*). Prompt and empty renders clear `lastResponse` so it cannot emit the previous scope; `_analyticsShowTableCard` is the only switch for the table card.
- **Default sort is `_anEffectiveSort`** (`task_number` in event scope, title globally, where `task_number` is null); `_anSyncSortHeaders` is the sole `aria-sort` writer.
- **Unique Attempters counts people**: excluded from `_anTotalOf`; zero attempters shows the rate as `—`.

### SPA conventions

ES modules under `public/js/`; module roles: see *Architecture*.

#### Module structure and boot
- **`core/state.js` is a dependency-free leaf exporting one mutable `S`.** Write `S.x = v`, never re-bind `S`: imported ESM bindings are read-only, so `S` is the only shared live state.
- **Module bodies are side-effect-free.** Delegated listeners, `registerAction`/`registerChange`/`registerInput`, IIFEs and the interceptor install live in `core/main.js` `boot()`, run once at DOM-ready, so no import wires anything twice. `core/dispatcher.js` holds only the handler registries and the a11y tagger.
- **Dispatchers run handlers through `_runHandler`** (`core/main.js`), which catches throws and rejections. Only click toasts the error; a toast per keystroke would cover the controls.
- **The tagger never gives a `TH` `role=button`** (it destroys columnheader/`aria-sort`); a sortable `th` gets tabindex only.
- **`boot()` rebuilds the `window` namespace** (test hooks, handoffs): `Object.assign(window, _ns0…)` plus live getters/setters for every `S` key. **A new module takes two edits:** `import * as _nsNN` AND adding `_nsNN` to the `Object.assign`; a by-name import in `main.js` hides a missed second edit.
- **`boot()` order:** `readEventVocabulary()` and `publishHeaderHeight()` first; `installFetchInterceptor()` before the first authenticated fetch. It strips `/join/<token>` or `?join=` via `history.replaceState({}, '', '/')`, keeping the token out of history and the Referer.
- **Import cycles:** `ui/nav.js` never imports `features/auth.js` (auth imports nav). `features/admin-crossnav.js` imports no feature module; it drives panes with synthetic `input` events. Use observers or registration-site wiring.
- **Participant routing goes through `enterCompetitionView()` / `routeVerdict()`** (`ui/nav.js`), including the `visibilitychange` resume; callers never re-derive the verdict. A refused branch never renders nothing: a `false` from `doLogin`'s auto-resume lands on `showMain('competitions-page')`, as `boot()` does.
- **`toggleAdminView` (`ui/nav.js`) rides a session regenerate:** it re-fetches CSRF and, going to participant view, stops every admin interval; a new one is cleared there too, or it 403s under a participant session (lock: `tests/admin-view-toggle.spec.js`). The Participant View button needs `S.currentUser.id`, and `Not a user session` signs out only a user-backed session: the built-in superadmin's login body has no `id`.
- **`_markAppTransition()`** (`features/impersonation.js`) sets `sessionStorage["ha:transition"]`; `theme-boot.js` consumes it before first paint and masks the login screen until `showPage`/`showMain` (an 8 s watchdog fails open). Impersonation enter and exit set it (a sign-in routes in place, no reload); the role-change and pending-countdown reloads and `announceImpersonationEnded` do not, so they flash login, as anonymous first paint and a manual refresh do by operator ruling (a persisted auth hint means a stale-hint spinner).
- **The signed-in header is reserved from first paint, layout only:** the `ha_shell` cookie (no Expires, no identity; `features/session-monitor.js` `setAppShellHint`) says what the browser last showed: `2` the signed-in header (`buildAdminNav`), `1` it plus the participant bar (a participant's sign-in, `lib/sessions.js` `setAppShellHintCookie` in `_signInAs`; `updateParticipantNav`), `3` the bar with its lock note on a line of its own (`buildParticipantNav`, `updateParticipantNav` while the note shows); cleared by `showPage`, a boot that finds nobody signed in (`ui/nav.js` `dropAppShellHint`: the static login page paints with no `showPage`; only on evidence, the session check's `active:false` or both identity reads answering 401, never a failed or refused read of a signed-in session), sign-out and the session-ended popup. `theme-boot.js` sets `html.app-header-hint` (any value), `app-shell-hint` (1, 3) and `app-note-hint` (3), and the CSS gives the still signed-out header the signed-in one's rules (each header-row, bar and note rule names its hint `… #app-header:not(.is-app)` beside `.is-app`; the still-`:empty` `#main-nav` gets the filled bar's box, and the account button's place is kept), so the header taking over moves nothing (locks: the reserved-row and phone-reload arms of `tests/app-chrome.spec.js`, layout shifts measured on a reload at 390 and 360 px, both views). Without it a signed-in reload moved `#main-content` (CLS 0.31 by the bar; on a phone 0.0486, and 0.146 in the admin console, by the signed-out header's own `#user-info` row). It never masks the login screen; a stale cookie costs the login screen only the frames before boot's session check answers (its hinted header hides "?" and the look picker; lock: the stale-cookie arm).
- **The client never reads `window.screen`, `window.orientation`, `orientationchange` or `device-*` media features:** Playwright forwards each device descriptor's unconfigured `screen` (lock: `tests/screen-inert.spec.js`; it also flags a local binding named `screen`, so rename it).
- **`theme-boot.js` reloads a page whose entry module graph failed to fetch, once:** never from a document that is itself an automatic reload (the one-shot `ha:boot-reloaded` marker, set before `location.reload()`, read and removed at the script's top), nor within 30 s of the last one (the `ha:boot-reload` stamp, compared with THIS document's start, never the failure's time, which can arrive 30 s late; both `Date.now()`, never `performance.timeOrigin`). No storage, no reload. One failed module request fails the whole graph, leaving the static login page painted with no handlers or OS-theme listener; the browser reports it only as an `error` event on the entry `<script type="module">` and the document keeps the failed module, so only a reload recovers. When it will not reload (spent, or no storage) it inserts `#boot-failure` (`role="alert"`, a Reload button) above the header, lifts `booting-authed` and sets `history.scrollRestoration` to `manual` (a restored scroll would carry the notice out of view). A SyntaxError is reported on `window` and never reloads or notifies (lock: `tests/boot-reload.spec.js`).
- **The checkers miss missing imports and stray backticks.** A missing `ui/` import passes `node --check` and esbuild and fails only at render (a functional-core-b lock catches it). A backtick in an HTML comment inside a template literal ends the literal, and `node --check` reports the wrong line. Bundle with esbuild after touching `public/js`.

#### Fetch layer
- **The interceptor (`core/api.js` `installFetchInterceptor`) answers a recognised sign-out refusal with a never-settling promise,** which no try/catch rescues. A request that must not hang goes on `skipPaths` (read it): the anonymous doors, the `/navigated-away` keepalive beacon (it lands after session transitions) and the session-replacing doors (`/api/impersonation/exit`, `/impersonate`, `/api/toggle-admin-view`).
- **Session-replacing doors also post via `_sessionTransitionPost` (`_origFetch`).** Each half suffices alone; both are kept and source-pinned. It refreshes CSRF *before* posting when `S.csrfToken` is empty and retries once on `csrf_invalid`. Keep that pre-flight: an unusable token reaching `csrfProtection` destroys the session.
- **One sign-out classifier:** `_classifySignout` + `_announceSignout` (`features/session-monitor.js`), shared by the interceptor and the 30 s status tick. Doors that bypass the interceptor (XHR uploads, `impersonateParticipant`, `_sessionTransitionPost`) call `_announceSignout` / `announceImpersonationEnded` themselves, or a raw machine token renders.
  - **A sign-out in another tab is named** (`signed_out_other_tab`), after any reason the server recorded (a signed-out session is never kicked or reset, so a reason proves the note stale): both sign-out doors share `signOutThisBrowser` (`features/session-monitor.js`: `readyToWrite`, which must succeed, then the marker, then the POST), whose marker `ha:signed-out-fp` holds the session's token fingerprint; only a tab whose own `S._csrfFp` equals it says so. It is withdrawn when the session is seen alive after a failed POST (a refusal or lost answer proves nothing) and whenever a token is issued for that session; the sign-out is single-flight, waits at most 3 s for its navigated-away row (which never opens the profile gate: `_PROFILE_GATE_SKIP`), and re-asks `readyToWrite` right before its POST (operator ruling, 21 Oct 2026; lock: `tests/signout-messages.spec.js`).
  - **`inactivity` is the last-resort verdict** (after `kicked`, `signed_out_other_tab`, `restart`, `unverified`): a failed probe, `csrf_invalid`, `active:true`, any status `reason` (unknown ones included) or no boot snapshot give `unknown` with neutral copy (a failed probe gives `signed_out_other_tab` when the note matches): telling a working user they were idle is the bug. The monitor's tick ignores an error answer (maintenance, a scanner block) rather than read a dead session.
  - **A 401 `session_ended` is handed to its caller, never announced**, after a `/api/session/status` probe: if the live session is this tab's own account acting as itself in the same view (`_liveSessionIsMine`, a `S.currentUser.id` required; another tab replaced it) `loadCsrfToken()` first, or the retry's `csrf_invalid` destroys the live session. Touch nothing otherwise: for another account or an impersonation the retry would commit as it, and for a dead session a refresh loses the kick context. **So is a 401 `Not authenticated` when that probe finds the live session this tab's own** (`_liveSessionIsMine`; the token re-synced): tabs share the cookie, so a request this tab sent just before another tab signed in again (the session regenerates) is refused on the session that sign-in replaced, and was taken for a sign-out of a signed-in browser (lock: `tests/csrf-cross-tab.spec.js`). Any other such 401 still signs the tab out.
  - `restart` needs `d.active !== true` and a `server_started_at` unlike the boot snapshot (`_sessionMon.serverStartedAt`, from `/api/config`). `GET /api/session/status` returns it unauthenticated, and must: a restart-invalidated cookie is unauthenticated.
  - Idempotence uses `S._signoutAnnounced`, not `_sessionMon.endedShown` (`stopSessionMonitor()` resets it): a second `showModal` settles the first as Cancel and redirects unread.
- **The redundant `/api/logout` POST after `csrf_invalid` stays** (functional-core-a lock; see *Accepted audit dispositions (intentional, do not re-flag)*).
- **`400 profile_incomplete`:** off `_PROFILE_GATE_SKIP`, the interceptor opens the gate for participants, then (string URL only) retries once with `signal` deleted (a `fetchTransientRetry` deadline has fired). Call sites stand down via `isProfileIncomplete`; `competition-picker.js` handles the refusal itself, admin tier included (see *Required user profile fields*).
- **Superadmin impersonation puts one shared confirm on every non-GET `/api/` write off `_impersonationWriteExempt`** (see *Roles, scope and impersonation*).
- **Each tab names the event it shows (`X-Competition-Id`, from `S.currentCompetitionId`); `requireCompetitionContext` and `select-timeslot` refuse another, or none (another tab went back to the list; only while the account is still in the named event or is staff, since an organiser's removal also clears the session's event and keeps its 400 and eject), with 409 `competition_changed` (`lib/middleware.js` `tabEventMismatch`, via `refuseBeforeBody`: the submit body is still unread).** Tabs share one session holding one event, so another tab's pick moved this tab's answers and penalties. Absent is unchecked (only the SPA has tabs); the interceptor is narrow, so a `Request`, `Headers` or array init goes unnamed. None is sent while this tab's own pick is in flight (`S._compSelecting`). **Every writer of `S.currentCompetitionId` bumps `S._compGen`, and the deselect POST goes only after the tab clears its event (scanner); leaving (the list, an eject) also bumps `S._compSelectSeq`, so a pick still in flight cannot put the event back**: a refusal whose generation moved is this tab's own and returns to its caller (reads drop it on their guard; an answer or hint shows the server's "nothing was recorded"); any other is announced once (`_eventChangedElsewhere`, polls stopped, a never-settling promise). The navigated-away beacon never announces (lock: `tests/cross-tab-event.spec.js`).
- **`X-Client-Active` is added only within 60 s of user intent** (`S._lastIntentAt`), only on a string `/api/` URL with no init or a plain-object init without a `Headers`; a caller-built `Request`/`Headers` is never rewritten.
- **`fetchTransientRetry(url, opts)` makes 3 attempts (400/800 ms backoff, 15 s `AbortController` each),** retrying a throw, 429 or 5xx. A final 429/5xx returns with no body deadline; a final throw returns `null`. Callers treat both as failure and leave the view alone. **Read its responses with `readBody(r)`, never `r.json()`:** fetch resolves at headers, so the deadline is handed to the body read (lock: test-only `/api/test/stall-body`).
- **Participant polls never eject on a transient failure.** "No longer in this event" needs a genuine membership answer.
- **A signed-out tab sends nothing, and a tab learns of a session's end from the session check, never a refused poll** (a room behind one NAT ending together, after a long break or a restart, otherwise passes the scanner block's 100 counted refusals, then cannot see its sign-out behind the block's 429s and re-blocks when it lifts). `_stopAllPolls` (`features/session-monitor.js`; every caller ends the tab's session) sets the terminal `S._pollsHalted`: every poll starter and the `visibilitychange` resume refuse, and the interceptor sends no same-origin `/api/` request (never settling); a new poll starter asks it too. It also writes localStorage `ha:session-ended`, as does `signOutThisBrowser`. The interceptor (`core/api.js`) asks `GET /api/session/status` (200, never counted) before the next request of a tab that shows somebody when the session is past its known expiry (`_sessionMon.expiresAt`; a fast client clock costs one check, never a sign-out), another tab wrote that key (`S._sessionCheckDue`), or the address was blocked: the block answers `429 {code:"address_blocked"}` with Retry-After (`server.js` `_scannerBlockMiddleware`), and until it passes no `/api/` request leaves, each answered locally with the same 429. The events page's poll also skips while the profile-completion gate is open, and an admin tier refused `profile_incomplete` there gets Try again, not a poll: each refused read counts. Lock: `tests/signed-out-polls.spec.js` (the block's code and the stale-cookie count: `tests/scanner-silent.api.spec.js`).
- **A participant in an event has ONE regular poll, the pulse** (`GET /api/participant/pulse`, `features/participant-tasks.js` `_pulseTick`): the /api/status body, the score pill, the board's `lb_version` and the live session's half of /api/session/status, so a 1000-tab event fits one thread with headroom (perf/RESULTS.md). It is a self-rescheduling `setTimeout` in `S.statusPollInterval` (15 s, 30 s once idle and unchanged, ±20 % jitter; never rescheduled after a stop nulled/replaced the handle, nor armed in a hidden tab: `_schedulePulse` records it paused in `S._pausedIntervals`, or a tab hidden at entry would never pulse). When the participant's own session ends while others run, `handleCompetitionEnded` stops it and `_pollWaitingForAllSlots` (15 s `setInterval` on /api/status) holds `S.statusPollInterval` until the event fully ends. Every other fetching timer is gated (held by review, not a scanner: grep `setInterval(` and self-rescheduling `setTimeout(` in `public/js`): the leaderboard poll (`_lbPollTick`) fetches only on a new version, each time the run ends further (`_lbEndStage`: a clock end bumps no version) or past `LB_FLOOR_MS` (60 s; the board also moves with time), counts a failed load as not loaded (`_lbLoadFailed`) and never reads /api/me; the session monitor reads a pulse younger than its interval instead of asking (a hidden tab asks at most every 2 minutes); an unverified account's verify ticker (`ui/nav.js` `startVerifyIndicatorTicker`, 30 s) reads /api/me every fourth tick, to clear its chip. The 1 s display ticks poll nothing (the countdown hands its end to `handleCompetitionEnded` once). Never add another fetching timer: extend the pulse (locks: `tests/participant-pulse-ui.spec.js`, `tests/participant-pulse-outcomes.spec.js`, `tests/leaderboard-poll.spec.js`, server half `tests/participant-pulse.api.spec.js`; `tests/session-slide-polls.spec.js` walks self-rescheduling timers as drivers).
- **Never write `fetch(u).then(r=>r.json()).catch(()=>[])`.** Use `fetchJsonList` / `fetchJsonObject` (`null` on failure) and render `null` as a failure. A 500's `{error}` is truthy, and fabricated fallback state lies.
- **`loadCsrfToken()` stores a token only on `r.ok` with `csrfToken` in the body;** overwriting a working token on a throttled refresh breaks the login card. `postWithCsrfRetry` serves the anonymous auth posts and **never sends without a token**: on a refused token fetch (429 behind a venue NAT) it waits (Retry-After, else capped exponential back-off with jitter, ~45 s at most) showing "Busy — retrying…" (the `ha:csrf-busy` event), then gives up WITHOUT posting (a local 503 `csrf_unavailable`): a tokenless post answers 403 `csrf_invalid`, which feeds the scanner tracker (lock: `tests/csrf-busy-backoff.spec.js`). **A sign-in door (code or proof redeem, password, break-glass, registration) regenerates the session, so it calls `adoptNewSessionToken()`**, which drops the dead pre-sign-in token and waits the same way; else a throttled refresh sends the first write with a dead token: the 403 ends the new session and its 401 polls block a venue NAT (same lock). When that wait gives up, `readyToWrite` (every interceptor write) waits for a token again rather than send none; one wait serves every caller and answers at once after its seventh ask (same lock; it holds the k6 login storm's `ensureToken` to it too).
- **The card's Continue asks `POST /api/auth/sign-in-method` first, except for a non-address, which gets the password box with no request** (`doLogin`, `features/auth.js`). A password account gets the password box on the card (Continue becomes "Sign in", "Email me a code instead" below it); editing the email box returns to the address step (`resetLoginStep`); every other address opens the code step (`openSigninCodeDialog`, which needs an address; there is no "Already have a code?" door). Continue for an address this tab was mailed a still-live code for (sessionStorage `ha:last-code`; lifetime from the server, `/api/config` `login_code_ttl_ms` or the shell's `<body data-login-code-ttl>` so a throttled `/api/config` cannot hide it, never a client copy; unknown, nothing is reused) reopens the code step without a new request, saying the code is already on its way; a redeem forgets it. The step never claims a code was sent (the answer is uniform); the operator's wording (14 Nov 2026): "If this address can sign in or sign up, a code is on its way. Personal addresses (Gmail, Outlook…) can't open an account without an invitation." Locks: `tests/login-method-ui.spec.js` (steps), `tests/signin-dialogs.spec.js` and `tests/signin-code-lifetime.spec.js` (reuse, lifetime), `tests/login-code.spec.js` (wording).
- **Every mutation reports its refusal.** `if (r.ok) {…}` with no else is a defect; a fallback to a removed endpoint hides the refusal behind a 404. Provisional counts say so: bulk toasts render `emailsDeferred`/`membersDeferred` as "not final".

#### Stale-response guards
- **Admin list loaders that a scope or search change can re-fire** take `const mySeq = ++S._xFetchSeq` before the fetch and check `if (mySeq !== S._xFetchSeq) return` before writing state (grep `FetchSeq`). The rankings guard precedes the `S._rkCache` write. A write that patches `S.adminCompetitionsData` in place (the event look's save, the event tag dialog and removal) then claims `S._compFetchSeq`, so a read sent before it landed cannot put the old row back (lock: the tag-race test in `tests/ui-events-groups-journeys.spec.js`, the look's in `tests/workspace-follow.spec.js`).
- **`S.myCompetitions`:** `loadMyCompetitions` and its poll claim and re-check `S._myCompsFetchSeq`; `ejectToCompetitionPicker` claims it before writing, so an old response cannot re-offer an event just left. A writer racing the poll must at least claim it (grep `S.myCompetitions=`).
- **Competition-scoped participant reads capture `S._compGen` before the fetch and re-check it after `readBody`.** `selectCompetition`/`chooseTimeslot` bump it; `S._compSelectSeq` orders the selection *write*. **`selectCompetition` also returns `false` when superseded,** so never eject on bare `false`: the waiting-page poll ejects only if `_compGen` moved by exactly its own bump and the view and event held.
- **Challenge views use `S._taskViewGen`, bumped by `openTask` AND by `showMain`/`showPage`; both are needed.** `_taskViewStale()` gates an *open*; `_taskStillShown(taskId, g)` gates an *outcome* (submit, hint, rating), which must not key on the generation, since reopening the same card is a new view. An outcome for a card no longer shown is toasted, never painted. `submitAnswer` has no post-confirm still-shown check by design: confirming is intent; after a solve's dialog it asks again, so whoever left the card meanwhile is not dragged back to the list.
- **A poll never overwrites a user's choice.** `loadTasks()` (the status poll's body) applies the default solve filter only while `!S.solveFilterUserSet`. The workspace status strip's 1 s tick skips repainting while focus is in the banner or a banner dialog is open; an explicit render meanwhile waits in `S._bannerExplicitPending`. The workspace Settings section paints its note, facts and Look card apart. The Look card's form follows the store in place only while it holds no choice not yet saved (each control's `data-saved` is the stored value it stands for; `_paintLookCard`, `setEventLookForm`); a card for another event, or whose editability changed, is drawn afresh, and leaving the workspace drops the choice. Its saves are counted per event (`look-admin.js` `_lookSaves`): while one is on its way nothing redraws the form, and an answer is applied unless a newer save's answer already was (lock: `tests/workspace-follow.spec.js`).
- **Snapshot-paged lists (Submissions, history) send `snapshot_tie` with `snapshot_before` and clear both together;** the server's strict filter needs both, and a leftover tie is worse than none. A Submissions sort change resets to page 1 and drops the anchor.
- **`_logsSyncPaging` (`settings-admin.js`) runs BEFORE the identical-entries short-circuit,** with the page bound from the response, never the DOM. An empty jump box does nothing; an over-range number clamps.
- **Dialogs that await before rendering take a per-dialog open token first** (grep `OpenSeq`), then remove any same-id overlay, bind controls via `overlay.querySelector`, and close only their own overlay. The help drawer re-checks `_helpLoadSeq` once, after reading the body.
- **A deferred listener arm re-checks that its target is still current.** `toggleKebab` arms click-outside in `setTimeout(0)` only while `portal._docCloseHandler === close`.

#### Lists, counts and load failures
- **Use the shared helpers, never copies:** `ui/list-controls.js` (list chrome), `ui/download.js`, `ui/profile-form.js` (both profile dialogs), `ui/task-search.js` `taskMatchesQuery` (every organiser challenge search; each whitespace-separated word must match the title, difficulty or a tag as a plain substring, never a RegExp built from the query).
- **Every data table on an admin PAGE goes through `ui/table-columns.js`; a table inside a dialog keeps its own search, sort and pages** (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026: the History dialog has all three, and the component's popovers sit on `<body>` at z-index 200, under a dialog's 3000 rung and outside its trap), unless `tests/admin-tables-component.spec.js` records a reviewed exemption: it demands `data-tcols` or a reasoned exemption for every `<table` the SPA writes, both ways (the import dialog's selection and conflict steps are forms, a tick or choice per row, not data tables; the geometry lock measures only opted-in tables on `#admin-page`). A table opts in with `data-tcols="<key>"` and a `defineTable(key, cfg)` from `boot()` (`CHALLENGE_TABLE_COLUMNS`, `PARTICIPANT_TABLE_COLUMNS`, `EVENT_TABLE_COLUMNS`, `SUBMISSIONS_TABLE_COLUMNS`, `LOGS_TABLE_COLUMNS`, `PLAIN_TABLES`). It hides columns by class (`tcol-off` on heading and cells, never in row renderers), stores the SHOWN keys per table in localStorage `ha:tcols:<key>` (Participants keeps `ha:p-cols`) and the unticked under that key plus `:off`, so a key in neither keeps its default and a choice made under one column set (a Logs stream, Rankings with names hidden) never hides a column another state draws (a shown list without `:off`, as specs seed, reads as exact; lock: `tests/audit-raw-logs.spec.js`), and adds a sort-and-filter button (`.tcol-hbtn`) to each heading and a `.tcol-bar` (Columns, filter note, Clear) before the table's card. A `model` table filters its whole data set before paging (`applyColumnFilters`, `applyColumnSort` in its renderer); any other filters the rows on screen and its note says "on this page". A heading that sorts itself keeps its sort and the menu drives it; a SERVER-sorted table (Submissions, Logs) names a setter, `cfg.sortTo(col, asc)`, so the menu sends one request for the order asked (page 1, anchor dropped), never a toggle clicked twice (Logs only while the server reports `sortable_globally`, else it reorders the page in hand, sending nothing; lock: `tests/audit-raw-logs.spec.js`); a heading the component sorts shows ▲/▼ (`.tcol-sort-ic`, `aria-hidden`, beside its aria-sort; sweep #316); the bar's Clear drops that sort with the filters (`clearColumnView`, "Clear sort"; an empty state's clear-the-filters button keeps `clearColumnFilters`), and a page-only table's rows go back to the order drawn (`_drawnAt`, which also breaks ties); a list drawn for another event (`data-tcols-scope`: the issued certificates, Team & reports) starts with no sort or filters (sweep #311); a DOM-sorted cell sorts by its `data-sort-value` if any (a time shown in words keeps its time there, read by a `date` filter too), a value is a number only when all of it is one (`_num`; text holding a digit compares as text), and `compareValues` puts numbers before text, a total order (lock: `tests/audit-pane-controls.spec.js`); any other heading is clicked, again at once if it sorted the other way, and re-checked after 60 ms only if unanswered, a newer press cancelling that check (`_driveNative`); the Events list keeps its default total order until a heading is chosen (lock: `tests/table-columns-server.spec.js`). **The default column set must fit a 1024 px window** (lock: `tests/table-columns.spec.js`, geometry at 1024/1280/1440): a new column or wider cell goes past the defaults, never back to a `min-width` on the table. The heading button sits in a reserved right strip of a heading at least 68 px wide, so clicking a heading's middle still sorts. **At <=768 px a `.table-card` table is cards and its headings are hidden (`visibility:hidden`: no tab stop; select-all stays), so the bar's Sort & filter (`.tcol-sf-btn`, shown on a `.tcol-cards` bar, and on every bar on a 769-1279 px touch screen, where a heading's button stays 24 px) lists the columns whose heading button a wide screen shows and opens that heading's own menu (`openSortFilterMenu` → `openHeadingMenu`); never a second sort or filter path** (locks: the Phone arms of `tests/table-columns.spec.js`; the v9.0.2 arm of `tests/cross-browser.spec.js`, where the popover's placement is clamped onto a short screen). Specs acting in a column past the defaults call `showAllTableColumns(page, key, keys)` (`tests/ui-admin-helpers.js`) before the first goto.
- **`paginationHtml(id, …)` renders only the bar, as stateless controls with per-surface action names** (`<id>-page-next`, `<id>-page-size`) that specs locate by. Surface differences (`lastEnabled`, `jump10`, approximate totals) are options, not forks. **The Audit page, Raw logs and Settings' CxOne API audit share ONE segment pager** (`ui/list-controls.js`: `segmentParams`, `segmentAbsorb`, `segmentStepTarget`, `segmentLastTarget`, `segmentGoTo`, `segmentBarOptions`, `syncPageJump`; each surface keeps a state object of `newSegmentPager`'s shape): page 1 read from the top and counted, later pages by anchor plus offset or a cursor Next was answered with, a read that answers no count keeping page 1's, Last and Go to page only with an exact count. Raw logs returns to page 1 only through `_logsFirstPage` (lock: the v7.0.2 arm in `tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js`), and `_logsSyncPaging` still runs before the identical-entries short-circuit; `syncPageJump` never writes the box's value.
- **Other table-component invariants.** **A table drawing each entry as its own `<tbody>` declares `unit: 'tbody'`** (the Checkmarx One removals card): the `<tbody>` is filtered, counted, sorted and listed in a heading's values as one, by its first data row, so a detail row never outlives its facts (lock: `tests/cxone-withdrawals-ui.spec.js`). **Rankings (every board) and Analytics are MODEL tables** (`RANKINGS_TABLE_COLUMNS`, `RANKINGS_GROUP_TABLE_COLUMNS`, `ANALYTICS_TABLE_COLUMNS`): a heading filter narrows the whole set before paging (before a board's top 50), in the rows the export writes; the Columns chooser never changes a file's columns. **The rankings boards rebuild their heading on every sort, so they name a setter** (`cfg.sortTo(col, asc, th)`, `setRankingsSort`; focus stays in the menu), and a heading rebuilt under its open menu hands the menu and `aria-expanded` to its new button (`_popAnchor`; lock: `tests/rankings-heading-menu.spec.js`). **While the component sorts a table, no native heading claims a sort** (aria-sort `none`, arrow cleared, unless the table is its own `ariaSortOwner`); pressing one takes over, ascending (`_driveNative`, which re-finds a replaced heading). **A row's actions column is always shown** (`_isFixed`). A column choice applies even where storage is blocked (storage only remembers it). **Both observers (`installTableColumns`, `installTableCards`) act only on the tables a mutation touched** (`tablesTouched`), since the workspace status strip repaints every second outside any table (lock: `tests/table-columns-units.spec.js`).
- **Sibling rule:** a search, jump or code input is a *sibling* of the container its handler re-renders (see *Recurring rules — the doctrine*). Search box, table shell and pager are static markup; loaders refill only the tbody. Polled grids (`#comp-grid`) hold no inputs, and no render writes a value the operator owns (`#logs-page-input`).
- **`wirePickerFilter` hides non-matching items instead of re-rendering,** so ticks and focus survive; it matches `textContent` plus `data-search`. `wireChipMultiselect` treats a caller-owned Set as truth, not `aria-pressed`.
- **Counts (`countHtml`, pure `countText`, `setListCount`):** `queried` gives "N of M match" for a typed query, "N of M shown" for chips or the default filter (chips fold into `filtered`); `loadedOf` marks a capped slice; `approximate` gives "N+"; irregular plurals need `nounPlural`. `setListCount` skips identical text (an aria-live node on polled tables). Paint the count on every exit from a render, early returns included.
- **A list emptied by a filter says so and names the filter.** A grid's empty state carries `.grid-empty` (`grid-column:1/-1`).
- **Every change that reorders or narrows the admin task list resets `S.tPage = 1`,** opening and closing an event workspace included.
- **The Participants pane always walks `/api/admin/participants`, never `/picker`,** inside a workspace too (the event's slot via `_focusedSlot` from each row's `competitions[]`); for Challenges, see *Challenges*. `/picker` caps non-members at the oldest 500 and sets `truncated`: its caller must surface that or narrow with `search` (Grant Attempts is safe only because it keeps `u.in_competition`).
- **Blanks sort last in both directions** by partitioning outside the direction flip (`_rkApplySort`, analytics `_anSortRows`); inside the comparator, descending makes it blanks-first.
- **`renderParticipants` clears `[id^="sort-"][id$="-icon"]` globally,** so other tables' sort icons use other prefixes (`lbsort-`, `rksort-`).
- **The Submissions page-scope note (`_subSortNote`) is empty on the default sort or one page;** on the default read `sorted_globally:false` describes the query plan, not the result. Hint and answer rows are separate render branches: change both.
- **Every admin pane loader renders `renderLoadFailure` into the pane on failure, not just a toast** (the Certificates section's two reads write the same alert with Try again themselves: `tests/certificate-pane-load-failure.spec.js`). Panes with no poll pass a `retryAction`; Events and Logs re-request themselves. A new pane joins `tests/load-failure-states.spec.js`. A failed read is `null`, never an empty list. Participant side: `loadTasks` renders Retry (`/api/tasks` has no poll), and a transient first `/api/status` failure retries once.
- **A polled table keeps each row unchanged at its place** (`ui/list-controls.js` `patchTableRows`, its pager `setHtmlIfChanged`; the Events list, whose 15 s poll renders whether or not anything changed): a replaced row loses a press that straddles the render and drops focus, so only a changed or moved row is replaced (lock: `tests/admin-row-press.spec.js`). The renderer still writes its markup through `setSafeHtml`, into a detached `<tbody>`, so the HTML-sink scanner judges it where it is built.
- **Never sort a canonical `S` array in place** (e.g. `S.adminCompetitionsData`); other surfaces look rows up in it.
- **New copy buttons use `ui/clipboard.js` `copyText`,** which falls back to `execCommand` without `isSecureContext` (venue LANs run plain http); only rankings uses it so far.
- **Every generated download goes through `downloadBlob`** (`ui/download.js`, scanner on `a.download =`), which revokes on a timer or `pagehide`, whichever is first: a synchronous revoke kills the download, `pagehide` alone keeps blobs for the tab's life. A certificate file names its holder through `fileNameSlug` there (letters of any script, accents kept; operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026), never a copy but its one server mirror, `lib/attachments.js` `fileNameSlug`, which names the top-three ZIP's files (body held identical: the sweep #72 arm of functional-core-b) (lock: Docs hygiene in `tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js`, where every Unicode property class in the client scripts is a listed decision; its limit: see *Stated gaps — what these do NOT cover*); event names in file names are ASCII slugs (naming them in any script is separate work, left undecided by that ruling).
- **CLS: reserve space card for card** (`showSkeletonCompCards`, 12 task skeletons), never a fixed height, only into an empty grid, and nothing when the count is unknown. Nothing tall goes below a container that fills after `/api/me`. A line that every load fills reserves one line (`1lh`) before its data lands: the Challenges page's `#tasks-subtitle` and `#task-grid-count`; the conditional `#presence-note` and `#cert-cta` reserve nothing (lock: `tests/cls-picker-skeleton.spec.js`).

#### Events, pickers and participant views
- **`COMP_CATEGORY_LABEL` (`features/competition-picker.js`) is the one status label table;** badge classes are `comp-${category}`, never per-site ternaries.
- **Admin start/end/duration reads `_compEffectiveBounds()`** (a multi-session event ends with its latest session), never inline arithmetic. The Competitions table orders by status band, then latest end, name, id: a total order, so the poll cannot reshuffle rows.
- **Admin event pickers sort with `compareCompsForPicker`**: live first, then upcoming by soonest start, then ended (an event taking late entries too) by latest start, then name and id, a total order (operator ruling, 24 Nov 2026: by start alone the oldest finished event sat above the next upcoming one). Analytics' event picker uses it too. Never "fix" them by flipping the server's `ORDER BY start_time DESC`, which others rely on (lock: `tests/event-picker-order.spec.js`).
- **The event-admin Role cell badges only live, upcoming and late-entry events,** with no label when there are none (operator ruling).
- **Participant confirmation exemptions read `staffSpectatorIn(comp)` (`ui/format.js`), never bare `S.isUserStaff`,** which would skip them for a competing staff member.
- **Picker, session and challenge cards are not buttons: each card's control is the `<button class="card-open">` in its title, named from the title first** (a whole card shows more words than a name can hold, and a button flattens the heading and ribbon list inside it). The card keeps its `data-action`, so a click anywhere on it still works; nothing else interactive nests in it. Leave (`#pending-leave`) is on the pending page.
- **Participant searches stay narrow** (tasks: title and category; events: name); wider matching leaks what the participant wire withholds.
- **Events inside an event is plain navigation: no "Leave Event?" confirm** (it still sends the deselect; operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026). **Start now keeps the scheduled end** (same ruling): a window kind's duration stretches to reach it, a deadline kind sends the start alone, a session keeps its end; start and duration come from ONE instant taken at the press, never before the confirm, the duration rounded up as End Now rounds (`startNow`; lock: `tests/ui-events-groups-journeys.spec.js`). "Switch Event?" remains, only when a competitor who left one live event picks another they compete in (lock: `tests/play-timer-ring.spec.js`, `tests/staff-compete-participant-ui.spec.js` (g)).
- **The play stats are ONE renderer, `playStatsHtml` + `paintGauge` (`features/participant-tasks.js`), with two homes:** the challenge view's play bar (`#qa-playbar`) and the events page's progress strip (`#ev-progress`, `_renderProgressStrip` in `features/competition-picker.js`): position track, time ring, score, and on the strip a Continue button. The strip lists at most three LIVE events this tab has played (`progressFor`, written by the task-list and score reads), is drawn from what the tab already read and asks the server nothing, and puts the run at the first challenge neither solved nor out of attempts (the record's `out`). `runStanding` (finished = solved or out, scoring challenges only) and `stripWords` judge it: with nothing left to answer the row says All solved or No attempts left (never a completion word), shows the solved count with no current segment (`over`) and offers View, the same door; it stays on the strip. Spent segments are hollow on both homes (`out`), and a spent challenge's list card says No attempts left (lock: `tests/next-challenge-out-of-attempts.spec.js`). On a multi-session event the run is the participant's own session (`my_session`, the window /api/status gives the play bar): its ring counts that down, and the run leaves the strip once it ends (lock: `tests/progress-strip-session.spec.js`). The ring's words adapt (`3d`/`18h`, a clock under a day, `42 s` in the last minute) and are fitted inside its inner disc (`_fitGauge`, via `--gauge-fit-fs`); a Program shows the track and Continue only. Lock: `tests/play-timer-ring.spec.js` (geometry at 360/390/1440).
- **The challenge view is the focus view on every width:** one clock (the play bar's ring; where it draws none, a Program, the bar says the time left in words, `.qa-when` painted by `paintWhen` in the events list's wording, `timeLeftWords`, and on a wider screen the header countdown and day chip stay too, a phone hiding the whole header; a late entrant's bar also says "Late entry"; every width, operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026; lock: `tests/play-bar-readout.spec.js`), Back as a close key, the header's score pill hidden wherever the play bar shows the score (owner's decision, 27 Nov 2026; lock: `tests/play-bar-readout.spec.js`), footer hidden, rating in the answer column; a solved or reviewed challenge shows keyed tiles (unchosen ones dashed and muted) and an inline Correct panel with points and Next challenge (the next one after it, wrapping round, neither solved, ended nor out of attempts: `_nextPlayable`). Keys (`_qaKeydown`): a letter picks; Enter presses the card's own Submit (multiple choice, pick-one) only from an option or from focus parked by script (the page, a `tabindex="-1"` target), so a link or control keeps its own Enter (WCAG 2.1.1; lock: `tests/challenge-view-keys.spec.js`). **The answer control stays in proportion to the question:** a text/any answer is one 48 px line, a JSON answer opens at five rows and grows as typed (`fitAnswerArea`, delegated `task-answer-fit`) up to a `45dvh` ceiling (40 on a phone), a file answer is the native input drawn as a compact dashed drop zone; every typing surface is at least 16 px and Submit sits under its control on screen at common desktop sizes. On a phone the fixed Submit bar is lifted above the on-screen keyboard by `--qa-kb` (`_keyboardLift`, visualViewport, only while `#ans-text` has focus) (lock: `tests/answer-box-proportion.spec.js`, geometry at 1440/1280/1024/390/360).
- **The leaderboard podium shows places 1-3 (each card names its certificate tier, Gold/Silver/Bronze, only once the board is final, only for points above zero and never on a dry run) and the table below starts at rank 4.** Nothing on either says how many people are on the board (see *What participants may see*).
- **Ended-event popup:** server `ended_popup_seen` may only raise `S._endedPopupSeen` (`S._endedPopupShownFor` remembers this tab's). `POST /api/dismiss-ended` is skipped under impersonation, or it sets the target's flag.

#### Modal dismissal contract
- **`overlayDismiss(overlay, fn)` (`ui/modal.js`) is the single close path.** A backdrop click never closes a dialog; the account menu and the command palette close on a press outside them (a menu and a search, where nothing is lost: operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026). Escape closes the topmost overlay via `window._modalEscStack` (LIFO, one listener, shared by the mobile admin drawer); remove any per-overlay Escape handler when adopting it, or both fire.
- **`showModal` / `_countdownConfirm` settle an open modal as Cancel before reusing `#modal-overlay`.** An orphaned promise wedges the interceptor's shared confirm.
- **`showModal` has no accessible-name option; the visible button text is the name.** `armMs` ignores activation briefly after open, so a double tap cannot confirm. `cancelText:null` hides Cancel; the confirm cannot be hidden, so relabel it (e.g. "Close") and do not read `ok` as consent.
- **Place every overlay on this ladder.** Rungs marked *inline* are set in JS `style.cssText`, so grep `z-index:` in `public/js` as well as the stylesheet:

| z | Element |
|---|---|
| 10000 / 9999 | `.touch-tooltip`, `.skip-link` / `.code-lang-menu` (inline) |
| 9700 | `#toast-stack` (stack `pointer-events:none`, each `.toast` `auto`) |
| 9600 / 9550 | `#modal-overlay` / `#admin-alert-overlay` |
| 9500 | `#self-enroll-overlay`; `#table-insert-overlay`, `#import-progress-overlay` (inline) |
| 9200 / 9100 | `#session-warn-overlay` (inline) / `.impersonation-banner` |
| 9000 / 8999 | rich-text link dialog (inline) / `#loading-overlay` |
| 4000 | `#help-drawer-overlay`; `#profile-gate-overlay` (inline) |
| 3100 | `#ts-form-overlay` (the session form), `showPrompt` (inline) |
| 3000 | ordinary dialogs, `.picker-overlay` (`#appearance-overlay` included), account menu `.pm-overlay`, command palette `.cp-overlay`, full-screen join QR `.ws-qr-full`; at <=768 px the quick-start sheet `.tpl-spin` (its scrim 2999) and the Filters sheet `.filters-panel` (its scrim `.filters-scrim` 2999) |
| 2000 | congrats overlay (`showCongrats`, inline) |
| 200 / 199 | `.admin-sidebar` drawer, `.color-pop`, dropdowns / `.admin-sidebar-overlay` |
| 160 / 150 | `.kebab-menu` / `.selection-bar` (the phone section bar is in flow, no z-index) |
| 100 | header (`#main-nav` is a row inside it), the phone Submit bar `.qa-submit-row` |

- **While a dialog is open the toast stack sits at the top** (`body.dialog-open`, kept by `ui/modal.js` `overlayDismiss`, every dialog's path): a dialog's primary action is in its footer, bottom right, and a toast there covered it while its hover-pause kept it up (lock: `tests/toast-dialog-placement.spec.js`; the event form's Finish creating in `tests/event-form-paths.spec.js`).
- **Reasons for the rung order:** `#modal-overlay` tops every dialog because confirms open *from* dialogs. Toasts top every scrim: a toast has no dismiss or history, so a covered one is lost. The hamburger sits *below* its drawer (above, it stole taps on drawer items). The Exit bar (9100) sits above the profile gate (4000) so it stays clickable. The full-screen join QR stays up on a stand's screen, so it sits on the dialog rung, under the session warning and the Exit bar (lock: the full-screen arms of `tests/join-qr.spec.js`).
- **Do not raise z-indexes to fix a sticky `thead` that shows under the header.** The table card is its own scrollport.
- **No admin-page box between the window and the workspace strip may be a scroll container.** `#admin-page.active` and `.admin-content` use `overflow:clip`, never `hidden`/`auto` (either makes that box the sticky scrollport, so the collapsed event strip never sticks and, at rest, is pushed down over the control below it), and outside Overview the workspace section is `display:contents` so the strip's sticky parent is the content column (lock: the strip arms in `tests/join-qr.spec.js`, and on every engine the workspace strip arm of `tests/cross-browser.spec.js`).

#### Modal button order + focus-ring
- **Buttons render Cancel then Primary in DOM order, in a row with `justify-content:flex-end`.** The primary takes focus on open.
- **Every `tabindex="0"` element has or inherits a `:focus-visible` rule.** Both rules are source-locked (Docs hygiene in `tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js`).

#### Overlay focus-trap + focus restoration
- **`installOverlayFocusTrap(overlay)` returns a remover.** `overlayDismiss` applies it; a MutationObserver releases it when the overlay leaves the DOM.
- **The trap ring holds only rendered, non-inert elements** (`getClientRects().length && !el.closest('[inert]')`), not an `offsetParent` test (null for fixed elements). `.focus()` on an excluded element silently does nothing.
- **The ring always includes the impersonation Exit button** (on `<body>`, outside every overlay), or a keyboard user behind the non-dismissible profile gate has no way out. The trap steps aside when nothing in the overlay is reachable, checked *before* Exit is appended.
- **Capture the opener before moving focus in.** `overlayDismiss` snapshots an opener outside the overlay and restores it only if focus is still inside or on `<body>`, so call it *before* focusing an inner control; its cleanup takes `{restoreFocus:false}` for an overlay that stays on screen as something else (a Filters sheet widened into an inline panel). `session-monitor.js` restores via `_sessionMon.warnOpenerEl`.
- **A re-render that replaces the focused control restores focus to it** (a rankings sort calls `th.focus()` after paint; the workspace status strip uses `_refocusBannerControl`; `renderAdminLogs` and a heading menu closing over a rebuilt header row (`_liveTwin`) focus the same control again; removing a filter chip focuses the chip now in its place, else the Filters button; locks: `tests/audit-raw-logs.spec.js`, `tests/filters-panel-ui.spec.js`). Sortable headers therefore emit `tabindex="0"` inline: the `_enableSortableA11y` observer tags a microtask too late. A `role="status"` node a poll rewrites returns early when unchanged.

#### Admin shell: sidebar and event workspaces
- **Events are run from their own WORKSPACE (`features/event-workspace.js`); there is no "focus" any more.** The Events list (`admin-competitions-tab`) opens one (`comp-open`, the row's name cell or Open): the compact two-line header `#ws-record` (a 64 px sticky strip on every section but Overview), the live status strip `#admin-scope-banner` (a `display:contents` box in the header's first line; 1 s tick: status chip and countdown, session chips, the ← Events leave button `scope-clear`; NO lifecycle controls), the KPI band `#ws-kpis` (Overview only) and section tabs `#ws-tabs`, each offered only to a role that may read it (`SECTION_PERMISSIONS` / `wsSectionOffered`; `wsTab` falls back to the Overview). The open event is `S.adminSelectedCompetitionId` / `S.adminSelectedTimeslotId`, which every admin loader narrows on; only the workspace's open/close writes them (`setAdminCompetitionScope` / `clearAdminCompetitionScope` underneath; a session chip opens `openEventWorkspaceSession`, whose `setAdminTimeslotScope` opening ANOTHER event resets the Search-everyone tick and the Challenges page as the event's own open does, while narrowing the open event keeps both: sweep #248). A clear from outside the workspace (Delete, the event gone from the model) leaves for the Events list (`syncWorkspaceChrome`), never a section with no header; the gone-from-the-model detector (`renderScopeBanner`) reads only a list the server answered (`S._adminCompsLoaded`), an empty one included, so an organiser's only event going is noticed and a failed load is not (lock: `tests/focused-event-deleted-ui.spec.js`). The server keeps no workspace state: shared sections send their panes' own requests, and the workspace-only sections call routes of their own (the Overview's `overview-pulse`, below).
- **Two kinds of door, decided in `ui/nav.js` `adminTab`.** A SIDEBAR item is a cross-event page and closes any open workspace first (`workspaceHooks.close`); a WORKSPACE tab (`ws-tab` buttons carry `data-section`, never `data-tab-id`, so a `[data-tab-id]` selector finds only the sidebar item; or `adminTab(btn,id,{keepEvent:true})`) keeps the event. The `WORKSPACE_ONLY_PANES` exist only inside a workspace; their loaders, like Templates', Roles & permissions' and Audit's, are registered in `adminPaneLoaders` from `boot()` (directly or by a module's init), so nav.js imports none of those modules. Crossnav (`admin-crossnav.js`) and "Manage participants" go through the workspace tab while one is open; crossnav also clears the target list's heading filters and widens People so its target shows (`pWsShowEveryone` in a workspace; outside one `widenPeopleToAdmins`, so that one entry lists admin accounts too, said by `#p-admins-note`, until People is next entered: People has no "Show admin accounts", admin accounts live on Roles & permissions, owner's decision, 27 Nov 2026; lock: `tests/workspace-crossnav.spec.js`). Specs: `clickTab` routes to the workspace tab while one is open; `viewComp` opens one.
- **Each workspace action has ONE place (operator ruling, confirmed 17 Nov 2026):** Invite is the header's one primary button, holding both ways of adding people (`wsInviteItemsHtml`: Email invitation, `ws-invite`, and Sign up at the desk, `desk-invite`, each by `wsInviteOffered`, the desk withheld with its reason by `deskApprovalWithheld`; owner's decision, 27 Nov 2026: inside a workspace the Participants section shows neither Invite nor At the desk, and the ⋯ menu no desk item), Approve beside it only while someone waits, the join link is its join pill, and every other door is its ⋯ menu (`wsMenuItemsHtml`), the irreversible ones last and quiet (Close late entries now, End now, Delete). Who runs the event is the Overview's Organisers card alone (no Organisers menu item). The Settings section keeps only Edit settings. **The header holds a render while a dialog that returns focus is open** (`_headerDialogOpen`; the strip's tick carries it out through `wsFlushHeldRecord`, which also repaints the header, tab counts and open section when what they show changed, never under a dialog: `_syncOpenSection`, `_ovSignature`; lock: `tests/workspace-follow.spec.js`), updates only the ⋯ items when the buttons are unchanged, and moves focus from a replaced control to the same one or the ⋯ trigger (lock: `tests/late-entry-close-ui.spec.js` U6–U6e). An ended, ranked event offers Download top 3 certificates: `GET /api/admin/competitions/:id/certificates/top3.zip` (`routes/export-import.js`, `scoped`; the issued placement PDFs at ranks 1-3 of EVERY board: one per session in per_slot mode, each in its own folder, the late entrants' in `late-entrants/`, each file `<place>-<holder>-certificate.pdf` with the name the certificate prints (`_certificatePdfFor`'s `holder`); missing ones are measured per board against the podium the mint awards (`placementPodium`, the mint's own `_mintEligibility`), never against "three", and named in `X-Top3-Missing` + `X-Top3-Missing-Reason`; 409 `event_not_ended` / `certificates_being_issued` / `certificates_not_issued_automatically` (`automaticIssueRefusal`, asked first even while a job waits: the dispatcher decides at run time) / `no_placement_certificates`; the certificates list carries the same refusal (`automaticRefusal`), which the Overview's card says in words instead of "being issued" (lock: `tests/certificate-surfaces.spec.js`); the Overview's Certificates card offers it by `top3Offered` (with `certificates.view`), the Events row's button by status and kind alone; each file audited `badge_certificate_downloaded` with `by_owner:false`, `via:"top3_zip"`; lock: `tests/join-qr.spec.js`). The Overview holds status, never a shortcut repeating a header control, and no leaderboard (operator ruling, confirmed 17 Nov 2026: the organiser acts on who is stuck, and the board lives in Rankings). Its Organisers card settles: the list, "No organisers yet", or a `renderLoadFailure` with Try again (`ws-organisers-retry`); only the read that started owns the card (lock: `tests/workspace-overview-final-board.spec.js`).
