- **The Overview is built around the organiser's next five minutes, decided by STATE and the wire policy keys, never the kind literal** (`_ovMode`: status, late window, `ranking_enabled`, `sessions`, `schedule`, walk-ups; `#ws-overview[data-ov-state]`). The attention row `#ws-ov-attn` comes first in every state (approvals; live, also nobody joined, challenges participants cannot see or nobody solves, else one "Nothing needs you right now" line). Upcoming: the "Ready to start?" checklist `#ws-ov-ready`, each open item with the control that fixes it (the ⋯ menu's own door, same `data-action`, or `ws-point` "Show me", which FOCUSES the header's Invite / join pill rather than repeating it), sessions still to run, the walk-up card. Live: `#ws-ov-progress` and `#ws-ov-rates` in one Progress card for every kind ("solved every challenge" from the pulse's `totals.finished`, then solves per challenge), the feed (card hidden while empty, `data-hide-empty`), sessions still to run, the walk-up card `#ws-ov-join` (`ws-ov-qr` reveals, then full-screen QR). Ended: final progress + `#ws-ov-certs` (one lazy `GET …/certificates?summary=1`, a success kept 60 s and re-read while a dispatcher job is due soon, a failure never kept, offering Try again; lock: `tests/certificate-surfaces.spec.js`), and the walk-up card while the late-entry window is open. A failed pulse refresh puts its notice and Try again in the attention row and withholds "Nothing needs you"; the checklist's challenges row and the Challenges tile claim nothing without a pulse and count scoring challenges only. Everything else (challenge set, chart, organisers; the solve rates while upcoming) sits in `#ws-ov-detail` behind ONE toggle `#ws-ov-detail-more` (`ws-ov-expand` part `detail`; specs use `openOverviewDetail`, `tests/ui-admin-helpers.js`). The KPI band leads with the countdown hero and holds only this state's figures, each a display number (the tab count chip is `.ws-tab .ws-count` only). Locks: `tests/workspace-overview-layout.spec.js` (KPI geometry, raised tab track, each state's face, no sideways scroll at 390), `tests/workspace-overview-states.spec.js` (what each state says), `tests/workspace-a11y.spec.js` (axe, every section, look and mode). Its live parts (the KPI band's Answers and Playing now, `#ws-ov-chart`, `#ws-ov-ticker`, `#ws-ov-rates`, `#ws-ov-progress`, the attention row, each challenge row's facts) come from ONE scoped read, `GET /api/admin/competitions/:id/overview-pulse` (`routes/event-overview.js`: scope before existence, then 404, then the competitor lock; Analytics' counting rules — answers from `challenge_events` only, `impersonated=0`, joins from memberships, `staff` out except staff enrolled in a staff-compete event, and, unlike Analytics, where staff do not compete also every role the boards leave out (`counted`: the boards' own list, `lib/user-tags.js` `offBoardRolesJson`, read once per pulse and bound by name as `@offBoard`, so every statement using `counted` passes it; operator rulings, 14 Nov 2026); no answer text or length — except that a qualifying question counts as an answer, never toward a solve or finishing; `solvers`/`finished` count current members, `active_now` and a task's `answerers` answers only; locks: `tests/workspace-pulse-counts.api.spec.js`; `tests/workspace-pulse-impersonation.api.spec.js`, impersonated activity in no figure and benchmark accounts in; `tests/competitor-lock-event-reads.api.spec.js`, the competitor lock, seat and hand), re-read by `refreshOverviewLive`, which rides the 15 s `startAdminCompPoll` and reads the pulse only while the Overview is on screen (each poll also repaints the tab counts and open section, `_syncOpenSection`): never give it a timer. **Joined, and every Overview line saying how many have joined, counts everyone enrolled**, like the Events list and the Participants tab (`_joinedCount`: the events model's `participant_count`, never the pulse's `joined`; shown at once, to a competitor too; `_syncRoster` repaints the band and those lines, each tracked apart, whenever what they last drew is not the model's roster); Progress's "of N" and the activity chart's Joins and summary stay the pulse's live figures (operator rulings, 14 Nov 2026). With nobody counted, Progress says "Nobody has joined yet." only when the roster and the read's `enrolled` are both 0 (as does the attention row), "Only staff and event admins (or roles like theirs) have joined" only when `enrolled` is the roster, else "Counting…"; an answered read whose `enrolled` is not the model's roster reloads the model (`_reloadModelIfBehind`) (lock: `tests/workspace-overview-joined.spec.js`). A competitor never asks for the pulse: their Overview says only that the live figures are kept off the page (`_COMPETING_OFF_PAGE`, never "not shown to you") and reads the certificate counts like any organiser, since every other read stays open to them (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026). Opening another event's workspace from an Overview renders the new Overview ONCE: nothing on the way (the scope change's `syncWorkspaceChrome`) may render it ahead of the open's own `wsTab`, since each render reads the pulse (lock: `tests/workspace-switch-reads.spec.js`). Lists open IN PLACE (`ws-ov-expand`, a "Show more" toggle AFTER each list, outside the container a repaint replaces; `ws-task-facts` flips one row's `<dl>` without a repaint), and every live repaint goes through `_paintKeepingFocus` (skips identical HTML, restores focus). KPI figures count up from what the tile last showed and flash on change (`_kpiShown`; an `aria-hidden` counting span beside the final value in `.sr-only`); reduced motion paints the final value at once. **Organisers are added and removed on the card** (`ws-org-add` / `ws-org-remove`; search through `GET /api/admin/participants/picker`, members filtered client-side, `truncated` surfaced), offered only by `_canManageOrganisers` (the route's own rule: not an event admin, `adminCan('events.organisers')`, not competing; the add picker also needs `participants.view`, the search's), whose refusal reason renders in the add picker's place; a pick asks first (Make organiser), since it can change a participant's role (lock: `tests/workspace-overview-live.spec.js`).
- **The join pill never reveals on its own:** the reveal (`GET …/self-enroll`) is audited and rate limited, so it happens on a press of the pill ("Join code"), which then shows the code and opens its popover; the reveal and the QR are cached for that open only, and a Regenerate replaces them at once (`admin:join-link-rotated`; a full-screen QR of the old link closes) (lock: `tests/workspace-doors-pressed.spec.js`: opening reads and records nothing).
- **Admin lists share one Filters pattern** (`features/filters-panel.js`): a Filters button opens a panel holding the list's chip groups (same ids and actions: `#comp-status-filters`, `#comp-kind-filters`, `#p-status-filters`, `#task-*-filters`), "Filters · N" and removable chips beside the button while any is active, Clear all. The open state is per browser (`ha:filters-open:<panel>`); specs that click chips directly call `openFilterPanels` (`tests/ui-admin-helpers.js`) before the first goto; at <=768px it does nothing, so a spec there presses Filters (lock: `tests/filters-panel-ui.spec.js`). At <=768px an open panel is a sheet with the dialog contract (scrim, `role="dialog"`, Escape stack, trap, focus back to its button), never opened by a page load whatever is stored (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026). Crossing the breakpoint closes an open inline panel (focus inside goes to its Filters button) and leaves an open sheet open inline, without the contract, focus kept.
- **Templates (on the New event page) are edited in full there by global admins** (`features/templates-admin.js`: New template, and Edit / Duplicate / Protect / Delete in each row's ⋮ menu (a built-in's Edit recommended setup / Duplicate as a template / Reset to default are in its kind card's ⋮ menu), Delete offered only on the wire's `can_delete`, else its `delete_refused` as a `.kebab-note`; a soft-deleted template is listed under Recently deleted (`#tpl-deleted`) with one Restore; grouped, with a live summary). Its challenges are listed and saved by difficulty, then title, unnumbered and never reordered by hand: an event numbers them that way whatever the template held (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026). An option the kind's policy forbids is disabled with the reason (`_forbidReason`) and never sent; the routes refuse the same options. Event admins get no template doors (lock: `tests/templates-editor-ui.spec.js`).
- **Settings is two panes, and its section list is DERIVED FROM THE MARKUP** (`settings-admin.js` `installSettingsShell` / `syncSettingsNav`): each `.settings-section[data-settings-section][data-settings-label]` in `#admin-settings-tab .settings-bento` is listed while it holds a `.settings-card` not `display:none`, one section shows at a time (the others `hidden`), and a MutationObserver re-derives the list as loaders show or hide cards. So a new card goes INSIDE a section (or carries `data-settings-section`; a card dropped loose into `.settings-bento` is adopted into General) and needs no list edit; never gate a card by hiding its section. **The list is grouped by each section's `data-settings-group` (`people`, `records`, `system`), captioned PEOPLE / RECORDS / SYSTEM (headings naming each group's `<ul>`, never buttons), and holds sections only: the pages the sidebar opens (People, Groups, Roles & permissions, Audit, the guides) are never repeated in it (owner decision, 27 Nov 2026):** People: Sign-in (approval, break-glass); Records: CxOne API audit (the Checkmarx One API activity table: `GET /api/admin/audit` narrowed by `event_type` to the Checkmarx One activity keys, `CXONE_AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES` from the Audit page's own `AUDIT_ACTIVITY_LABELS` less `cxone_config_changed`, so each row is scoped as the Audit page scopes it; the shared segment pager and `PLAIN_TABLES['cxone-audit']`; and the removals card); System: General (default Checkmarx One, retention, alerts, event team report domains), Branding (platform name, platform look, certificate design), Email (SMTP) (the mail server with its one test send and one relay status (Last check), the unsubscribe list; the Outbound email card shows only to a role holding `ops.email` without `settings.view`, which cannot read the mail server card: never both, owner decision, 27 Nov 2026), About & health (`health`: "This server", every figure from `GET /api/admin/system-health`, and the newest version of the release notes, rendered by the drawer's `renderDocMarkdown` with their headings moved under the card's, offered by `releaseNotesVisible`). No card shows a placeholder: a figure the route cannot give says why (an unknown build, backups not visible). The chosen section is a preference (localStorage `ha:settings-section`): while it has nothing to show the first listed one is shown, and it returns when its card appears (the break-glass card loads late; `open-builtin-account` and the removals banner select theirs first). Phones get the `#settings-section-pick` `<select>`. Specs acting outside General call `openSettingsSection(page, key)` (`tests/ui-admin-helpers.js`); lock: `tests/settings-two-pane.spec.js` (every section reachable alone, ≤ 1.5 viewports at 1440×900, the adoption rule, phone 390, the grouped menu of sections only and where the moved cards live); `tests/settings-about-health-ui.spec.js` and `tests/about-health.spec.js` (each figure by value, a release build and a mounted backups folder included); `tests/cxone-api-audit-ui.spec.js` (planted records by value, decoys absent). **Each card follows its routes' permissions, and the Settings item opens for any of them** (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026): `ui/nav.js` `SETTINGS_CARD_PERMISSIONS` (a card shows to a role holding every permission its routes check, plus the read's for a card that edits) and `settingsOffered` (tier fallback until `S.permissions` is known; never under an organiser's impersonated seat), so a regular admin sees the certificate designer, the mail server (read-only), team-report domains, Retention, Alerts, the CxOne API audit and About & health. Each card gates ITSELF in its loader, never by the built-in tier (`settingsCardOffered` for the cards in that map, the look card's inside `renderPlatformLookCard`; `certificateDesignOffered` for the designer; the team-report domains and break-glass cards have their own), starting `display:none` in markup so nothing flashes before the read; a new card joins `SETTINGS_CARD_PERMISSIONS` or gates itself. A role without `settings.view` never reads `GET /api/admin/settings`, so its cards with routes of their own load at once; for a role with it they load after that read, and a failed read hides them and the cards showing its values behind the one failure (the certificate designer, loaded first from its own route, stays) and drops the removals card's Review focus (`tests/cxone-withdrawals-ui.spec.js`; locks: `tests/rbac-client-permission-map.spec.js`, card ↔ routes both ways; `tests/settings-admin-certificates.spec.js`, `tests/settings-cards-permissions.spec.js`, by heading).
- **The People profile card (`features/profile-card.js`, action `participant-view-profile` with `data-id`: the row's View button and the kebab's View profile) is read-only and adds no scope of its own**: it renders the People row (whose `competitions[]`/`groups[]` the server already narrowed) and, only with `certificates.view`, asks certificates for the six latest of that row's events that can hold one (ended, or live with wire `certificate` `completion`: a Program issues while live), stating a negative only about what it checked (lock: `tests/profile-card-certificates.spec.js`); its doors reuse the kebab's predicates (`impersonateOfferedFor`, `profileReadOnlyFor` in `participants-admin.js`). Lock: `tests/profile-card.spec.js` (decoy event + superadmin control; seen failing with the participants-list `_compScope` clause removed).
- **Approval UI appears only when there is approval work:** the Pending chip shows while `S.requireAccountApproval` (from `GET /api/admin/me` `require_account_approval`) is on, someone is actually pending or it is active (so it can be turned off again); the workspace's Approve action and the attention row's approvals line only while someone in the event is pending.
- **`#admin-sidebar` items are static `.admin-nav-item` buttons** with `data-action="admin-tab"` + `data-tab-id="admin-*-tab"`, in three captioned groups (Manage, Participants: People and Groups (`admin-groups-tab`; the page was called Teams, renamed by owner's decision, 27 Nov 2026; the workspace's "Team & reports" is another thing), Admin: Roles & permissions (`admin-users-tab`) and Settings; New event is `admin-templates-tab`). Submissions and Logs have NO sidebar item (Audit covers both; the workspace Activity tab is Submissions): the Logs pane is the Audit page's second view, behind its Raw logs switch (`audit-admin.js` `auditSetView`), either view keeping the Audit item current, and pressing the view shown does nothing (re-entering Raw logs would reset its pager). Audit always opens on the trail, never the view last used (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026). Lock: `tests/audit-raw-logs.spec.js`. Both panes keep their ids; specs open them through `clickTab`, which calls `adminTab` for those two. Each item is hidden when the role lacks the permission its pane's first read needs (`ui/nav.js` `ADMIN_NAV_PERMISSIONS` / `adminCan`, from `S.permissions`, re-read from `GET /api/admin/me` on every nav build), and an empty group's caption with it; the command palette's Go to follows the visible items and its actions ask `adminCan` too. `adminCan` takes a list, any of which opens the page (New event: `templates.view` or `events.create`), and answers no outside an admin view. **The panel opens on Events only for `events.view`, else on the first offered page (`openAdminLanding`); every pane loader and poll asks its own permission first** (a refused read toasts and counts towards the address's scanner block). **A door asks the permission of its route and of each list its dialog reads: an unreadable list is left out with a sentence saying so, a dialog without its writes opens read-only with the reason, and a name links to another page only when `adminPageOffered` (`ui/nav.js`) allows** (lock: `tests/rbac-custom-role-ui.spec.js`, one arm per door beside a role holding what the first lacks; nothing enumerates the doors, so a new dialog read or write needs its own arm). Labels are copy; select by `data-tab-id`. Specs rely on `participants-tab-btn`, `pending-badge`, `admin-sidebar-toggle`, `admin-mobile-menu-btn` (visibility by CSS only) and `admin-sidebar-overlay`. Collapse persists in `localStorage.adminSidebarCollapsed`; items carry `title` only while collapsed.
- **Specs treat admin as ready once `#admin-page` is visible, never once nav items attach;** the items exist before `/api/me` sets `S.isSuperAdmin`.
- **`initAdminSidebar()` clones the toggle, the mobile hamburger and the overlay,** so handlers look them up by id at click time; a held reference goes stale.
- **Phones keep the drawer, not a bottom bar.** The drawer is modal: opening is idempotent, it makes `.admin-content` (a sibling) AND the section bar `#admin-mobile-menu-btn` (outside it) inert, installs the trap and pushes onto the Escape stack. Every exit calls `_closeAdminSidebarMobile` (releasing both inerts before handing focus back to the bar): `adminTab()`, `showMain`/`showPage`, Escape, the scrim, widening past 768px. **At <=768px `#admin-mobile-menu-btn` is the SECTION BAR: full width, in flow at the top of `#admin-page` (never fixed or floating, and not sticky: `#admin-page` clips its overflow, so a sticky bar sticks to that box, measured 61 px low), naming the section on screen (`#admin-section-label`, `_syncSectionBar` in `adminTab`/`buildAdminNav`).** The content reserves no band; route-focused headings and `#ws-title` carry a `scroll-margin-top` of header + bar. The scrim ignores presses for 500 ms after the drawer opens (`S._sidebarOpenedAt`): the second tap of a double-tap on the full-width bar lands on the scrim, not the brand strip (lock: `tests/admin-mobile-menu-overlap.spec.js`, geometry in `tests/app-chrome.spec.js`). Inside a workspace the tab strip becomes the Section button (`#ws-section-pick`, a sheet), and beyond Overview the header collapses to its first line.
- **No admin page scrolls sideways at 360/390 px.** Every admin data table in a `.table-card` on `#admin-page` becomes one card per row at <=768px with a label gutter; a table outside one (Groups' `.table-wrap`, Live rankings, Analytics, the Checkmarx One removals list) keeps its headings and scrolls inside its own box; the labels are the column headings, written onto each cell as `data-label` by `ui/table-cards.js` (a MutationObserver on `#admin-page`), never by the row renderers. A cell spanning columns gets none. The heading row shows only its select-all box (`th.cb-cell`, `data-label=""` so the caption never labels a card); sort and filter go through the bar's Sort & filter (see *Lists, counts and load failures*).
- **Row kebabs (44×44) portal to `#kebab-portal` and are the only row-action path at ≤768px.** `toggleKebab` measures the real menu off-screen before flipping or clamping it. The workspace header's ⋯ uses the same portal, filled by `wsMenuItemsHtml(c)` (`features/event-workspace.js`). **The Events-row menu (`compMenuItemsHtml(c)`) holds only Delete event** (owner's decision, 27 Nov 2026: every event is run from its workspace; Open and Top 3 are the row's buttons, and a row with nothing to offer draws no ⋮), with the workspace's predicate and label (lock: `tests/menu-door-parity.spec.js`, a state × viewer matrix: the row offers nothing else, Delete agrees, neither offers Organisers, the workspace keeps every door the row gave up).
- **Every admin pane has its own `h2`,** because route focus targets `h1,h2` (the workspace-only panes' are `.sr-only`; inside a workspace the shared panes' titles are visually hidden), unless the pane names its target (`data-route-focus`, `ui/nav.js` `_focusTarget`): the Overview's is the event's name, `#ws-title`, as its own heading sits below the header and KPI band and focusing it scrolls a short screen's header off the top. Focus moves when the PLACE changes (the pane, or the event it shows: opening another event on the section on screen is announced), never for the panel's own landing (`adminTab(…, {quiet:true})`; it would strand the skip link). A route-focused `h1/h2[tabindex="-1"]` lands below the header and any strip stuck under it (`--strip-bottom`, *CSS custom properties*) and draws no outline (`:focus{outline:none}`); real `:focus-visible` rings are untouched (lock: `tests/workspace-open-focus.spec.js`, the landscape-phone arm of `tests/cross-device.spec.js`).

#### App chrome: header, account menu, participant bar
- **Signed in, the header holds only Search (`#app-search-btn`), ONE round account button (`#profile-menu-btn`) at the far right and the live chips (timer, day, late entry, verify, score).** `showMain` adds `#app-header.is-app` (`showPage` removes it), the key of CSS section "THE APP CHROME": `#user-info` becomes `display:contents` so its children join the header row; `#theme-slot`, `#release-notes-slot` and `#platform-version` hide. `#help-slot` (`help-drawer.js` `_helpMountIcon`) holds "?" (`#help-icon-btn`) and Search; CSS shows "?" signed out, Search signed in, so anonymous specs click "?" and signed-in ones open help via the menu (`tests/chrome-helpers.js` `pressAccountMenuItem(page, "help-open")`) or the palette. Search is named by its word (the shortcut hint is `data-glyph`; on a phone the word is clipped, never `display:none`). Signed out, beside "?" sits only the look picker (`#look-picker-btn`, alone in `#theme-slot`; `features/look-picker.js`), whose dialog is the visitor's one mode control (no day/night toggle): a locked look keeps it; it goes only when the mode is locked too, leaving nothing to offer (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026; `ui/theme.js` `_modeLockReason`). The boot-readiness signal is `<html data-booted="1">`, set by `boot()`'s last statement and nowhere else (lock for both: `tests/signed-out-header.spec.js`).
- **The account menu (`openProfileMenu`, `ui/nav.js`; `#profile-menu-overlay`, a compact popover under the button, a bottom sheet at <=600px) is a short list offering items BY CAPABILITY:** the view item (`viewSwitchOffered()`: `data-action="toggle-admin-view"` with `data-on`; an admin tier enumerated positively AND user-backed, `S.currentUser.id`), ONE Appearance row (`open-appearance`, naming the current look · mode; specs reach its page via `tests/chrome-helpers.js` `openAppearance`), Profile (user-backed), Account (`builtinAccountOffered()`: the built-in superadmin; opens the Settings break-glass card via `open-builtin-account`), Help (`help-open`, everyone), What's new (`releaseNotesVisible()`), Sign out last. In Admin View the running version is NOT in it: it sits at the admin sidebar's foot (`#admin-sidebar-version`, "Version <v>", "v<major.minor>" while collapsed with the full text kept for screen readers, filled from `/api/config` by `settings-admin.js` `showSidebarVersion`; owner decision, 27 Nov 2026; lock: `tests/settings-about-health-ui.spec.js`); the participant view, which has no sidebar, keeps it small beside Sign out (`.pm-ver`, read from `#platform-version`; lock: the Version display arms of `tests/ui-participant-b.spec.js`). Items are delegated actions; the menu closes SYNCHRONOUSLY in its own click listener, before the document dispatcher runs the item, so the item's dialog captures the account button (a live node) as its opener. "?" tips sit BESIDE rows (`.pm-row`), never inside a button. The scrim closes it (a menu, like the kebabs); Escape and focus return go through `overlayDismiss`. The button's face (initials painted from `data-glyph`, emoji or picture) is decorative; its name is its `.sr-only` text, and `data-role` carries the tier. Lock: `tests/app-chrome.spec.js` (incl. panel height ≤ 70% of a 1440×900 viewport).
- **The command palette (`features/command-palette.js`; `#command-palette-overlay`, a full-screen sheet at <=600px) opens from Search or Ctrl/⌘+K** (`installCommandPaletteKeys`, signed in only, not from a contentEditable, not over another modal dialog: one on the Escape stack or a shown `[aria-modal="true"]` like the profile gate, which is on no stack). Sections Go to / Actions / Events / Help rebuild from live predicates per render: organiser Go to is the sidebar items whose `<li>` the nav builder left showing, plus the open workspace's `#ws-tabs`; Actions take each control's own predicate (`+ New Event` for global admins only, Invite and Sign up at the desk as the header offers them (`wsInviteOffered`, the desk also `!deskApprovalWithheld()`), `wsMenuItemsHtml(c)` for the open event MINUS its `km-danger` doors); Events read `S.adminCompetitionsData` / `S.myCompetitions` (no new endpoint), status via `compDisplayCategory`; a participant's event opens by leaving for the list as Events does (`handleCompetitionsNav`), then `pickCompetition` once that deselect is answered (`S._leaveInFlight`), so one the list's filter hides opens too; Help is `help-drawer.js` `helpDocHeadings(path)` over `helpDocsForRole()`, the drawer's own substitution + renderer into an inert document, so a result's id is the drawer's anchor (`openHelpDrawer(path, {frag})`). Items run by pressing the real control or dispatching its `data-action` via an attached hidden button (a detached one does not bubble to `document`); focus returns to the opener BEFORE the item runs. Recents (localStorage `ha:cp-recent`) are ids re-resolved against the gated list. The input is a sibling of `#cp-list` (combobox + `aria-activedescendant` over a listbox). Lock: `tests/command-palette.spec.js` (role gating by value with a superadmin control, mutation-checked: `_adminView` forced true, `_globalAdmin` without its event-admin clause, the hidden-`<li>` filter removed; also the workspace's doors, a participant's jump and the profile gate).
- **The participant bar is ONE row (`#main-nav`: Events / Challenges / Leaderboard) under the header row on every device; the header is sticky, so it never scrolls away, and there is no bottom tab bar.** All three items always render; `updateParticipantNav` marks Challenges/Leaderboard inactive (`aria-disabled`, `.nav-btn-locked`: dashed edge, muted ink, never opacity; `data-action="nav-locked"` toasts the reason) with a visible `#nav-lock-note` saying why (no event, not started, unranked). Only the full-screen challenge view steps it aside (`releaseTapChrome`). Empty (signed out, before boot, the admin console) `#main-nav` is `display:none`, or its base rule's 52 px `min-height` would leave a blank box in the header. Lock: `tests/app-chrome.spec.js` (geometry at 1440 and 390).
- **Anonymous boot asks `GET /api/session/status` first and, on `active:false`, skips `/api/me` and `/api/admin/me`** (no 401s in a signed-out console). A failed or odd probe falls back to the identity reads.

#### Appearance and the org default look
- **`features/appearance.js` is the Appearance page (`#appearance-overlay`; `open-appearance` → `openAppearancePage`): mode, both looks (live previews), ambient colours, "Show all tips" and the org default look for a role that may change the Settings Platform look card (`settingsCardOffered("platform-look-card")`, one gate for both doors).** Choices are per browser, none on the server: mode in `hck-theme` (`system|light|dark`, `system` stored explicitly; `ui/theme.js` `_applyThemePreference`, lock `tests/theme-cycle.spec.js`), look in `hck-look`, ambient colour in `hck-ambient`. Under a lock the look controls become a note (`_lookLockReason`), here and in the signed-out look picker, as do the Mode controls under a platform lock that sets a mode (`_modeLockReason`); the signed-out picker follows the locks on every repaint (`_syncLookPickerBtn`: a join landing's event lock arrives after the header mounted), since a choice that cannot apply is not offered. The organisation default reads the look in force (`_orgDefaultLook`, never a remembered save) and its save redraws the Settings card, so neither door puts an old look back.
- **THE PLATFORM LOOK (`lib/platform-look.js`, `settings.platform_look` JSON `{look, mode, ambient, lock, stamp}`) rides every page that runs `theme-boot.js` as `<html data-platform-look>`** (`server.js` fills `{{platformLook}}` in the SPA shell and `help.html`, part of the frozen-shell key; email-link pages and the public certificate pages (`routes/badge.js` `_badgeShellTop`) put `platformLookAttr()` on their `<html>`), so `theme-boot.js` paints it before the first frame; the email pages' one palette follows its mode and lock (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026; lock: `tests/platform-look-pages.spec.js`, whose source arm fails a `theme-boot.js` page without it). Never move it to an after-boot fetch: the default would reach a browser only on its second load. `PUT /api/admin/platform-look` (superadmin, strict validation, audited `platform_look` with `reqContext`); Settings card `#platform-look-card` (`features/look-admin.js`). `default_look` is its look field: legacy `PATCH /api/admin/settings {default_look}` writes through it (that route saves ONE setting per request: naming two of `platform_name`, `require_account_approval`, `default_look` gets 400 `settings_one_at_a_time`, as each branch writes only its own); a legacy `settings.default_look` row is read while no platform look is stored and dropped by the first write; `GET /api/settings/appearance` returns both.
- **Precedence, in `theme-boot.js` and `ui/theme.js` `_appearanceSources` + `_lookPreference` (one rule, two copies; change both): a LOCKED event look while inside that event; a LOCKED platform look unless this browser is staff (`hck-staff`, set by the nav builders from the role, positively); this browser's own choice unless its `hck-look-v` is older than the platform `stamp` ("Apply to everyone now"; theme-boot drops such choices once); an unlocked event look; the platform look; the house default, Ambient in the device's light or dark mode (`HOUSE_LOOK` = `"ambient"`, `HOUSE_MODE` = `"system"`, part of the flat-design plan the operator approved, 13 Nov 2026).** Every personal setter writes `hck-look-v` = the stamp in force. Lock: `tests/platform-look.spec.js` (first paint with the app module blocked, Apply, lock, staff control, event look).
- **THE EVENT LOOK (`competitions.event_look`, `event_look_locked`) has its own door, `PUT /api/admin/competitions/:id/look` (`routes/event-look.js`: 404, competitor lock, `competitionDenied`, then past the effective end only a superadmin, 403 `event_look_ended_locked`, the Settings Look card read-only by the same `canEdit`; no `updated_at` bump; History `look_changed`)**, rides `_PARTICIPANT_COMP_COLUMNS`, the admin list and the token preview, and is painted through `ui/theme.js` `setEventLook` (sessionStorage `hck-event-look`, so a reload inside the event paints it first) by `ui/nav.js` `_syncEventLook` (every `showMain`/`showPage`) and, on the join landing, `features/self-enroll.js` `_renderJoinBanner`; every session end clears it (`features/session-monitor.js`), as boot does for a signed-out load with no join pending, or a shared booth tablet would show the next visitor the last event's look (lock: `tests/event-look-signout.spec.js`). Clone, templates and export skip it (no competitions importer). With no colour stored, Ambient lights Ice (`DEFAULT_AMBIENT`, mirrored in `theme-boot.js`). **Four looks were retired (operator ruling, 13 Nov 2026):** `db.js` (marker `looks_retired_at`) moved a stored platform, legacy or event Heritage, Command or Checkmarx once to Signature; a platform Hyperscreen, however stored, to Ambient at night (a legacy `default_look` row in force becomes that `platform_look` row; operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026), an event's Hyperscreen to Ambient with no mode, since an event look has none (lock: `tests/platform-look.spec.js`); a browser's own stored retired look reads as no choice, and the routes refuse a retired id by name (`platform_look_unknown`, `event_look_unknown`).

#### "?" help tips (`ui/help-tips.js`) — the API for every screen
- **Markup: an empty placeholder `<span data-help="area.key" data-help-label="Filters"></span>` where the tip belongs.** `installHelpTips()` (boot) wires every placeholder, present or rendered later (one MutationObserver): a 24 px "?" button (44 px target under a coarse pointer via its `::after`; `data-action="help-tip"`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`, named "About <label>", glyph from `data-glyph`) and its `role="note"`, hidden until pressed. A renderer that rebuilds its container re-emits the placeholder.
- **Text: the `HELP_TIPS` table, or `registerHelpTips({ "area.key": "1–2 sentences." })` from a feature module at boot.** A value may be `{ participant, admin, event_admin, superadmin, default }` for role-specific copy, **re-worded once the role is known:** static placeholders are wired at boot, before it is, so both nav builders call `refreshRoleTips()` to re-resolve each per-role tip (lock: `tests/rbac-custom-role-ui.spec.js`). A key with no string renders nothing; `registerHelpTips` re-wires a placeholder wired before its text arrived, so registration order does not matter. "Show all tips" (Appearance) sets `hck-tips-all` and `<html data-tips-all>` and opens every note.
- **The tip is a SIBLING of the title or label it explains, never inside it** (wrap both in `.help-label-row`), so a heading's text and a control's accessible name stay their own; a placeholder hidden with its control needs its own `hidden` (People's scope checkboxes). Every key the source names must resolve for every role, and every visited screen's tips must render with text (lock: `tests/help-tips-coverage.spec.js`, which also fails a tip inside a heading, label, legend or other named element, in source or on screen).
- **The Audit page's Activity column shows `AUDIT_ACTIVITY_LABELS` wording** (`features/audit-admin.js`; an unknown key shows spaced and capitalised); the raw key stays in Details and as the CSV's "Activity key". The search sends the keys whose wording matches as `q_event_types`, which the route ORs into `q` only. A new recorded event type needs wording (scanner: `tests/audit-activity-labels.spec.js`, which also lists the writers whose type is a variable); a certificate record (`badge_*`, `certificate_*`, `credential_*`) kept on an event's or a person's History also needs History words in `features/history.js` `formatEventChange` (scanner: `tests/history-certificate-wording.spec.js`).

#### Help drawer role routing
- **Default doc by role** (`_helpDefaultDocForRole`): superadmin `admin.md`, event admin `event-admin.md` (tested before `isAdmin`), admin `admin.md`, everyone else `participant.md`.
- **`_helpMemoryValidForRole` rejects unknown files,** so release notes never become the remembered default.
- **Release notes reuse `openHelpDrawer(docPath, {title})`; there is no second drawer.** Their doors (What's new in the account menu and the palette) ask `releaseNotesVisible()` (`S.isAdmin || S.isSuperAdmin`); `renderReleaseNotesButton` still fills the hidden `#release-notes-slot` with `#release-notes-btn`.
- **The drawer is a `div[role=dialog]` with a `div` head, never `aside`/`header`;** an `aside` with that role would promote a nested `header` to a second banner.
- **Cross-guide anchors:** `public/help-renderer.js` emits slugged heading ids (marked emits none) and keeps `#fragment`; `_helpLoadDoc(path, frag)` scrolls the drawer itself (`location.hash` does nothing there) and re-aligns as each screenshot above the heading loads, until the reader moves the drawer; `/help.html` scrolls after the async load. `{{platformName}}` is filled by one helper (`_helpSubstitutePlatformName`, shared with the palette) BEFORE rendering, because a slug comes from the substituted text.

#### Date/time formatting
- **`fmtDt(v, shape, opts)` (`ui/format.js`) is the only date formatter.** Shapes `'short'` (date), `'long'` (date and time), `'full'` (default, with seconds); `withZone` appends the IANA zone, only for timestamps someone must act on before a deadline.
- **`fmtDt` keeps exactly one `return d.toLocaleString(undefined, fmtOpts)`,** the scanner's exemption. The scanner also skips the `shortOffset` tz probe and argument-less `toLocaleString()`; any other date `.toLocaleString(`/`.toLocaleDateString(` fails. Fix the site, not the scanner.
- **Wall-clock conversion goes only through `wallClockToUtc` / `utcToWallClock`** (`data/country-tz.js`), which sample the offset at the resulting instant and never throw. `utcToWallClock` returns `null` for an unusable zone; `wallClockToUtc` reads the wall clock as UTC as a fallback, `null` only for a malformed date or time.

#### Icon convention
- **Every icon is inline Feather-style SVG from `ui/icons.js`:** `ICO` (14×14, `stroke=currentColor`). No emoji or webfont icons.
- **The only exception is the `&#9998;` entity on the Challenges Draft chip.** The Docs-hygiene `FORBIDDEN` array is authoritative.
- **`#header-timer` has no icon;** its gradient text would clip an SVG.
- **The text and display stacks name the colour emoji fonts** for user content (the fallback is latin-only). Their lock only checks the names; a glyph measurement would be vacuous in CI.

#### CSS custom properties
- **THE LOOKS: the palette is generated, never hand-edited.** `scripts/theme/looks.js` holds every look × mode (Signature and Ambient, the house default) and Ambient's six colours; `npm run theme:tokens` (`scripts/build-theme-tokens.js`) writes the block between `/* BEGIN GENERATED LOOK TOKENS` and `/* END … */` in `public/styles.css` and exits 1 on any text pair below AA; `tests/theme-tokens.spec.js` holds the block to the data. Selection is `html[data-look][data-theme][data-ambient]`, set before first paint by `theme-boot.js` (whose `LOOKS`/`AMBIENTS` tables mirror `ui/theme.js`); `[data-stage]` renders the look's night mode. Design contract: `.claude/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`.
- **Legacy token names are aliases** (one generated rule from `LEGACY_ALIASES`): `--surface2`→`--surface-2`, `--accent2`→`--accent-ink`, `--green`/`--badge-green-fg`→`--ok`, `--red`/`--badge-red-fg`→`--danger`, `--yellow`→`--warn`, `--*-soft`/`--*-pill`→`--*-soft`, `--shadow-N`→`--sh-N`; the font names `--font-sans`/`--font-body`/`--font-label`→`--font-text` and `--font-num`→`--font-display` for one release only. **`--accent` is the GRAPHIC accent** (ring, glow, step mark): a fill carrying text is `--accent-strong` with `--on-accent`, accent text is `--accent-ink`, a status fill's label `--on-status` (but the bright warning fill `--warn-strong`, the Pending chip, takes `--on-warn`), and an accent tint is `rgb(var(--glow) / a)`, never a literal violet, which stays violet under every other ambient colour (lock: `tests/css-token-sites.spec.js`).
- **A per-look rule keys on the element that carries the look when it can be nested** (`.lp-mini[data-look="x"] …`): `[data-look="x"] .y` also matches through the page's own look and restyles every preview.
- **Use tokens; palette values live in `public/styles.css`, with no `@import`,** because a failed import silently breaks every `var()`. Never `color-mix()` (older WebKit drops it); write literal `rgba()`/`rgb(r g b / a)`.
- **Type comes only from the type-token block** (the hand-written `:root` opening "TYPE — the one block", above the generated one): `--font-display`/`--font-text`/`--font-mono`, role sizes `--fs-<role>` with `--lh-*`, steps `--fs-12`…`--fs-64`, weights `--fw-regular`/`--fw-semibold` (`--fw-score` only on the score figure). The root stays 16 px (no body clamp); every `th` is the eyebrow (12/16 600 caps); buttons never uppercase; a `<button>` with no class inherits its container's size (the bare `button` rule), so one meant to look like a button is a `.btn`. **No literal font-family, font-size or font-weight in `styles.css` outside that block or in `public/js/**`** (lock: `tests/type-scale-scanner.spec.js`; a geometry-derived size (the avatar emoji's `--av-fs`, the time ring's `--gauge-fit-fs`) rides a custom property so the declaration stays a token reference; `FONT_SIZE_CEILING` 11 counts only the standalone unsubscribe/verify sheets). Scale table: `.claude/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §2.
- **Corners come only from the radius tokens** (`--r-s`/`--r-m`/`--r-l`/`--r-xl` by role, `--radius-sm`, `--pill-radius`), because a look differs by its radii: no literal on the ladder in `styles.css`, `public/js/**` inline styles or the shell's markup, and any other literal is a recorded decision with its reason (lock: `tests/radius-tokens.spec.js`, which derives the ladder from `scripts/theme/looks.js`). Roles and decisions: `.claude/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §4.
- **A0: a per-look night rule never keys on a bare `:root[data-theme="dark"]`**: (0,2,0) out-ranks every other look's (0,1,0) blocks. Signature's night applies to `:root:not([data-look])[data-theme="dark"]` and `[data-look="signature"][data-theme="dark"]` (lock: `tests/theme-tokens.spec.js`).
- **Flat Design 3.0 governs construction; a look changes colour and corner radii only** (operator ruling, 13 Nov 2026; `.claude/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` principle 4, §7a): soft shadows from `--sh-1/2/3` only; no bezel, glass, inset key lighting, `backdrop-filter`, blur filter or blurred accent glow (a flat translucent ring with no blur is fine); no smooth gradient but the countdown text, loading shimmer and data rings (lock: `tests/theme-tokens.spec.js` "Flat Design 3.0": every blurred `box-shadow` layer is an `--sh` tier, gradients against an allow-list with reasons, stale entries fail). Medals are flat discs from `--gold-strong`/`--silver-strong`/`--bronze-strong` with measured labels; a leaderboard row's medal icon is the metal ink (`--gold`/`--silver`/`--bronze`).
- **A colour is a look token in both modes, never a literal tuned to one**: outside the generated block no colour literal in a mode rule (`[data-theme]`, `[data-stage]`, `prefers-color-scheme`), no translucent white or black (a hover is the `--surface-3` well), no translucent literal in a ring or keyframe, and no white text, and no translucent white or black, in a `public/js` inline style; reviewed exceptions sit in `ONE_MODE_ALLOWED` with a reason, checked both ways (lock: `tests/css-token-sites.spec.js` 5).
- **The standalone shells (`public/verify-pages.css`, `public/unsubscribe.css`, for pages that cannot load `styles.css`; the maintenance page's own `<style>` block too) name the shell's tokens and declare the ones they use:** `--r-s`/`--r-m`/`--r-l` at Signature's values, each by its §4 role (cards `--r-l`, buttons and inputs `--r-m`; never a `--radius*` name or a literal radius), `--shadow-1`/`--shadow-2` for BOTH modes at the §5 tier values (every shadow one of them, no gradient), header and footer `--surface` (DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §4, §5, §7). A tier declared for one mode only paints that value in the other mode too. Locks: the standalone arms of `tests/theme-tokens.spec.js` (shadow, radius, and the header and footer in a browser); the v6.10.7 arm of `tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js` (values per mode).
- **No `backdrop-filter` or `transform` on the header, cards or any ancestor of a phone-fixed element** (the selection bar, the challenge view's Submit row): it becomes their containing block.
- **Difficulty uses green/amber/red by operator decision.** Every status ink clears AA on `--surface2` and its tint in every look (the generator measures it; axe scans the app's screens in every look and mode: `tests/looks-app-a11y.spec.js`), so the light `--badge-*-fg` remediation rules resolve to the same ink.
- **Late-entry badges use the brand purple pair (`--brand-ink`/`--brand-soft`), not Upcoming's amber;** the pair is fixed per look and mode, never the accent, which an ambient colour replaces (with Amber the accent is Upcoming's hue, with Emerald Live's; lock: `tests/theme-tokens.spec.js`). The participant's `.header-late-entry` chip stays amber on purpose.
- **Hover and focus text must reach 4.5:1 in every look, day and night, with every ambient colour** (lock: `tests/interactive-contrast.spec.js`, resolving each combination via `tests/look-tokens.js`). Resting `--muted`/`--text` hovers to `--accent2`, resting `--accent2` to `--text`; `--accent` is never hover text. A hover rule that paints onto its own fill states the `background` in the same rule, or the scan measures it against a card; such a tint is its component's own check (a row menu's danger and warning tints, a token by day and night: `tests/css-token-sites.spec.js` 1c). Buttons lift 1px on hover and change fill or shadow; they never scale or fade (lock: `tests/hover-pulse-no-fade.spec.js`).
- **Height reservations read the measured `--header-bottom`, never a constant.** It is the header's border-box bottom edge: `watchHeaderHeight` observes the BORDER box, as the signed-in header (`is-app`) trims only its padding, which a content-box observer never sees. `--imp-bar-h` is the impersonation bar.
- **A strip a page keeps stuck under the header carries `data-sticky-strip`** (the workspace's `#ws-record` on every section but the Overview, the challenge view's `#qa-playbar`). `publishStripBottoms` (`ui/nav.js`) publishes its stuck bottom edge on its page as `--strip-bottom` right before every route focus (a ResizeObserver answers too late for the first section after the Overview), and again on a resize and on every new `--header-bottom`; the route-focus scroll margins read it, so a heading and the controls under it land below the strip. The strip must have its final height by then: the play bar paints its clock before `showMain` (lock: `tests/workspace-sticky-chrome.spec.js`).

#### CSS rules
- **Never dim text with `opacity`** (see *Recurring rules — the doctrine*), nor a control's glyph (the search glyph and a menu item's icon idle as solid `--muted`; no `svg` or `::before`/`::after` takes an opacity between 0 and 1, lock: `tests/css-token-sites.spec.js` 2b), nor from JavaScript through any expression: a script never fades a disabled `.btn`, which `.btn:disabled` draws at full strength (scanner: `tests/js-opacity-scan.js`, run by functional-core-b Docs hygiene; a value it cannot read fails); use a dashed edge plus `--muted`, and override the global `[aria-disabled="true"]{opacity:.5}` on text surfaces (axe exempts aria-disabled from contrast), as the page bar's buttons and Go to page box do (lock: the same spec's 1l). **Every disabled field (any input but a checkbox, radio or range, file and colour included; a select; a textarea) keeps its own fill with four dashed `--border-strong` edges and a `--muted` value, placeholder and file button (GrayText under forced colours), from one doubled-`:disabled` rule beside the base field rule** (operator ruling, 25 Nov 2026; an inline style still beats it, and an input drawing its own text, `.ha-code-input`, opts out); the locked Schedule switch is dashed, its chosen option `--surface-3`/`--text-2` (locks: 2d; `tests/disabled-fields-ui.spec.js` on four real screens). No other rule in the three stylesheets (keyframe stops aside) and no style attribute in `public/*.html` takes an `opacity` or `filter` `opacity()` between 0 and 1 unless `OPACITY_ALLOWED` records it with its reason (lock: 2c, both ways). Muted rows dim per cell (`cd`/`cellDim`), never by row class, second `class` attribute or `style=` (scanner-pinned). Unpickable rows (`.picker-row.is-disabled`, `#gc-list`, `#assign-task-comp-list`) follow that at `opacity:1`; only their box stays faded.
- **A `.form-group` caption is its classless `<label>`** (`label:where(:not([class]))`); a classed label inside a form group (`.checkbox-row`, `.picker-row`) keeps its own layout, so never widen the caption rule to every `label`.
- **Every rule or inline style that uses `var(--font-mono)` also sets `font-variant-ligatures:none`, and code `pre` blocks do too:** JetBrains Mono ligates `=>`, `==`, `!=`, `--`, `://` into single glyphs, and code must show the characters typed (lock: `tests/code-blocks.spec.js`: a source scanner over first-party CSS, HTML, JS and `routes/`, plus an editor pixel witness). The rich editor also keeps spellcheck and autocorrect off on `code`/`pre` (`_CODE_LITERAL`, `ui/richtext.js`; never stored), or WebKit's smart punctuation rewrites `--` and quotes as real characters.
- **Every `vh` declaration has a `dvh` sibling.** Form controls are at least 16px on coarse pointers, via doubled selectors so source order cannot break them.
- **Gate tappable hover on `@media(hover:hover)`.**
- **A `:has()` rule stands alone, with its fallback inside `@supports selector(:has(*))`;** one unsupported selector drops the whole list.
- **Scroll containers that hold `.sr-only` text are `position:relative`.** Wide content scrolls in its own container, never the page.
- **Media queries add no specificity,** so source order decides. A longhand comes after any shorthand for the same property.
- **Any author `display` beats the UA `[hidden]` rule,** so restate `X[hidden]{display:none}`.
- **Reduced motion is `transition:none!important`,** not a tiny duration, which makes `getComputedStyle` lag a frame.
- **A transition's timing is the motion tokens, each item naming both** (`--duration-*`, `--ease-*`; left out, they are 0 s and the browser's `ease`), in `styles.css` and in inline styles from `public/js`; the rise curve is `var(--ease-rise)`, and a fixed timing is one `.claude/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §6 names (lock: `tests/css-token-sites.spec.js`).
- **Checkbox labels use `.checkbox-row`.** `.result-msg.wrong` means graded wrong only; other outcomes use `.notice`. Checkboxes and radios reset input chrome with `revert`, never `none` (an invisible box). Two recorded exceptions use `none` (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026): the challenge card's drawn answer key (`#task-detail-card.qa-card .choice-option input`) and the editor's switch (`#admin-task-form .ce-switch input[type="checkbox"]`), each drawing its own box and, under `forced-colors`, its checked state in `Highlight` (lock: `tests/forced-colours.spec.js`); copy neither. Search inputs omit `type="search"` (its native ✕ doubles Clear).
- **Dead-CSS sweeps keep `hljs-*`** (added at runtime). Code-block CSS is mirrored onto `#task-preview-overlay`; change both, never regex them into `:is()` lists (lock: the frame arm of `tests/code-blocks.spec.js`).
- **Rich-editor popovers are `position:fixed`, placed by `_placePop()`** (`ui/richtext.js`); `.rich-editor` (`overflow:hidden`) must never gain `transform`/`filter`/`contain`, which would clip them.
- **The header platform name is shown whole or hidden, never wrapped or ellipsised** (`header h1{min-width:0}`; `initPlatformNameFit` observes the h1's size and the name's text, never attributes, which would loop). **The h1 never shrinks below the logo:** `min-width:min-content` with `contain:inline-size` on the name, so the floor is the logo alone (both under `@supports`; without the containment the name holds the h1 open and the header wraps early). Only once `#main-nav` has content (`:has()`; signed out, no nav or live chips sit beside the logo), and a hidden name keeps its empty box, so showing it never moves the floor. With no room left the header wraps one row rather than run the nav over the logo (lock: `tests/header-name-fit.spec.js`). Logo-size overrides must carry the `.logo-dark`/`.logo-light` class, or the base 36 px rule wins.
- **Desktop `.selection-bar` is `position:sticky` in an unbounded scroller, so on long lists it sits below the fold by design;** only the phone/coarse-pointer bar is fixed, so derive viewport assertions from the device, not computed style.
- **Inline `<style>` is CSP-blocked,** so test with `element.style`. The minifier keeps whitespace around `+` and `(`/`)`, so write `calc()` and `@media … and (…)` with spaces (see *Architecture*).

#### Accepted touch-target exception
Targets are 44×44 except for these rulings:
- **`.btn.sm` at 32px:** `.reset-link-actions-inline` in `#self-enroll-overlay`, and `#self-enroll-overlay .self-enroll-code-row` (44px full-width at ≤600px).
- **At the WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 24px floor** (dense, admin-only, keyboard-operable): `.invite-group-pick`/`.bulk-group-pick`; `.tag-x` (aria-label names tag and target); `.cb-cell input[type=checkbox]`; `#admin-rankings-tab .rk-copy` (a real, always-visible button); `.rk-player-link`, `.sub-link`, `.rankings-card-view`, `.log-details-btn`; `.chip-more` ("+N", operator ruling, 24 Nov 2026).
- **`.tcol-hbtn` is drawn at 24 px; under a coarse pointer its `::after` is a 44 px target inside a 48 px strip** (heading at least 96 px wide and 44 px tall), up to 768 px for the tables that keep their headings and from 1280 px for every table (operator ruling, 24 Nov 2026; locks: the Touch arm of `tests/table-columns.spec.js` probes 390 and 1280 px, and its fit walk runs under touch at 1280 and 1440 px). A 44 px `::after` on a 24 px button with a 1 px border is `inset:-11px` (its containing block is the 22 px padding box). From 769 to 1279 px the button stays 24 px under a coarse pointer and every table's bar shows Sort & filter (44 px, the same menus: WCAG 2.5.5's equivalent control; operator ruling, 24 Nov 2026; lock: the 769-1279 arm of the same spec).
- **`.rich-toolbar button` is 30×28 on fine pointers,** 44×44 under `(max-width:768px),(pointer:coarse)`.
- **`.link-btn-inline` sits mid-sentence,** under SC 2.5.8's inline exception.

`grep -nE 'min-height:(2[0-9]|3[0-9])px|height:2[0-9]px' public/styles.css` also finds unruled sub-44 controls (e.g. `#admin-sidebar-toggle`, `.tag-add`, `.color-sw`): unresolved, not precedent.

#### Dev server
- **The dev server runs nodemon, not `node --watch`** (no ignore list). nodemon keeps its starting environment, so a newly required env var needs a nodemon restart. Watch lists and restart costs: DEVELOPMENT.md.

## Deployment and CI image

**Podman servers pull, never build:** `.github/workflows/release-image.yml` builds the image on a GitHub-hosted runner, boots it read-only with no capabilities until `/readyz` answers, and pushes it to GHCR signed with the sigstore key whose public half is `deploy/podman/signing.pub` (`stable` on main and `v*` tags). `deploy/podman/install.sh` (Ubuntu, once) installs a Quadlet unit that refuses unsigned images for that repository only, backs up the database before every start, and follows `stable` through `podman auto-update` with rollback. Its runner breaks the functional-core-b rule that every workflow uses `cx-private-ubuntu-x64` (an operator decision pending). The checkmarx.yml test jobs below still build nothing. **Whenever `package-lock.json`, `Dockerfile` or `scripts/prune-runtime-deps.cjs` changes, build the image and boot it before the old container comes down:** scratch `DATA_DIR`/`UPLOADS_DIR` volumes, `BASE_URL`/`SMTP_HOST` set, wait for `/readyz` (recipe: the boot step of `.github/workflows/release-image.yml`). The build fails closed; the documented redeploy builds before it replaces the old container, so a failed build leaves the old version serving.

### App image (`Dockerfile`)

- **Both stages use the same `FROM node:<major>-alpine`, never `FROM --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM`.** The prune keeps what loads where it runs, so another libc or architecture keeps the wrong binaries. A Docs-hygiene lock holds the two `FROM` lines equal.
- **The build stage runs `npm ci --omit=dev`, then `node /tmp/prune-runtime-deps.cjs /app/node_modules --apply`.** The `/tmp` copy never reaches the image and, running after `npm ci`, does not invalidate the dependency layer. `RUN rm -rf /src/scripts` removes the context copy.
- **The prune script fails closed.** `--check` is the default and deletes nothing (exit 0 clean, 1 residue, 2 refused); only the Dockerfile (and the spec, on a temporary copy) passes `--apply`, which refuses a symlinked tree, a directory not named `node_modules`, or one next to a `.git`. Run it with plain `node`, never `npm run`: npm exports `npm_config_*` variables that node-gyp-build reads.
- **The prune keeps what actually loads.** A scrubbed-env child process exercises each native dependency, because a bare `require` proves nothing. Deletions come from a per-package table, never a sweep. Anything the table does not cover (an unloaded native binary, a `BUILD_INPUT` file, a rule hitting a loaded binary, a failed probe) exits 2 with nothing deleted, so a bump that adds a native package fails the build: teach the table (lock: `tests/prune-runtime-deps.spec.js`).
- **Image self-check.** The last `RUN` before `USER node` fails if any of `gcc g++ cc c++ make python3 python node-gyp npm npx` is on PATH, a `BUILD_INPUT` pattern remains under `/app/node_modules`, or `/app/scripts` or `/usr/local/include/node` exists. It matches exact names (`node-gyp*` would match the runtime loader node-gyp-build). Its `find` list must stay in step with `BUILD_INPUT`.
- **The image has no npm.** The runtime stage deletes npm, npx, corepack, Yarn and `/usr/local/include/node`; nothing at runtime needs npm and its bundled dependencies were scanner findings. `docker exec … npm` does not work. `apk` stays.
- **The runtime stage runs exactly `RUN apk upgrade --no-cache` (locked).** `--simulate`, a package argument or `--self-upgrade-only` would upgrade nothing.
- **The code tree is root-owned and read-only to `node`.** Final-stage `COPY`s have no `--chown`; only `DATA_DIR` and `UPLOADS_DIR` are created and chowned to `node`, after `WORKDIR` and before `USER node`. The build stage's `chmod -R u=rwX,go=rX /src` stops a restrictive host umask producing an image that crash-loops on EACCES.
- **`.dockerignore` is an allow-list:** `*`, then `!` lines for what the image runs. A Docs-hygiene lock derives the needed roots from the code and both Dockerfiles and requires an exact two-way match; a sub-path is allowed only if it is exactly a file the app Dockerfile `COPY`s. Known limit: a stray file inside `public/` or `docs/` still ships.
- **`docker-compose.yml` sets `build.pull: true` and `pull_policy: build`,** so every build re-checks the base digest, at the cost of needing Docker Hub.

### Redeploy

- **Clear the cached `apk upgrade` layer.** On an unchanged base digest it is reused and ships the first build's Alpine packages; neither `image prune -a` nor `build.pull` clears it.
- **Order (README, Docker):** `git pull`, `compose build --pull --no-cache`, `up -d --no-build`, *then* `image prune -f -a` (before `up` it deletes the image just built), then an optional `builder prune -f`. Never `compose down` first: a build that fails then (an Alpine branch move at the pinned `apk add`, a registry outage, a prune refusal) is an outage (sweep #128; lock: Docs hygiene in `tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js`).
- **The build stage's `apk add` pins `~=` versions on purpose (a KICS finding closed by it).** An Alpine branch move fails that line; re-pin it from `apk update && apk policy python3 make g++` in the new base, never unpin (README, Docker: "When the build stops at `apk add`").
- `scripts/backup.sh` runs on the host (see *Import, export and backups*).

### CI image (`ci/Dockerfile.cross-browser`, `ci/README.md`)

- **Every workflow `image:` line pulls `mpogr/hackarena-ci` by `@sha256:` digest;** the tag comment is informational. Find them with grep `image: mpogr/hackarena-ci@sha256`, not a count. A rebuild re-pins every line, because each job's "Link pre-baked dependencies" step compares the baked `/ci-deps/.lock-hash` with `package-lock.json` and fails before any test runs.
- **Rebuild on any `package-lock.json` change, a Playwright bump or a production Node major bump.** `ARG NODE_MAJOR` equals the app Dockerfile's major (lock: `tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js`). `@playwright/test`, the `FROM mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v<X>-noble` tag and the workflow tag comments move together (lock: `tests/ci-config-lockstep.spec.js`). Confirm MCR has the amd64 manifest first; arm64 can land earlier.
- **The build-time check is its own `RUN node <<'CHECK'` after the `npm ci` RUN** (folding it in changes the cache key and re-uploads the large layer). It exercises better-sqlite3 and bcrypt, checks every default `browsers.json` entry is installed at its revision, and launches chromium (headless shell and full build), firefox and webkit, checking each version. Workflow link steps also open `new D(':memory:')`, because `require('better-sqlite3')` loads no native code.
- Node is downloaded as `.tar.gz`; the base has no `xz`.
- **Pushing:** build with `--provenance=false --sbom=false`. If `docker push` dies on a large layer, push through the registry v2 API (ci/README.md). Never send a large upload through a shared SSH/SOCKS tunnel; it starves `gh`.

### Dependency bump, and why it ships WITH the CI image

- **The lockfile change and the CI image rebuild go in ONE commit;** a lockfile edit alone turns every test job red at its drift guard.
- **Check the npm cooldown before rebuilding:** the org's StepSecurity "NPM Package Cooldown Check" (not in this repo) cancels every job if a moved package is under two days old, after an upload that takes hours (check `npm view <pkg>@<ver> time`). Signs: `cancelled` jobs and a failing "StepSecurity Required Checks" naming the package.
- **A PR that edits a workflow (every CI-image re-pin does) gets no CI:** StepSecurity's `block-secret-exfiltration` run policy cancels each job at "Set up runner" because the file references `secrets.CX_*`, until someone other than the PR author adds the `workflows-approved` label; it does not apply once merged. Merging without that label is the operator's call.
- **Order:** bump, re-pin `package.json` `allowScripts`, build, verify (new lock hash, production Node major, loadable better-sqlite3), push, run CI's drift guard against the pulled digest, re-pin every `image:` line, merge.
  - `allowScripts` entries (`name@version`) match the lockfile; a bumped package with an install script needs one. better-sqlite3 needs one despite declaring no install script, because its `binding.gyp` makes npm synthesise `node-gyp rebuild`.
- `npm run bump` does not touch `package-lock.json` (see *Commands*).
- **A clean `npm audit --omit=dev` does not prove a bump unneeded;** upstream fixes can predate advisories. Read changelogs, especially for request-path packages.

### CI jobs (`.github/workflows/checkmarx.yml`)

- **`checkmarx-scan` is the only required check on `main`;** the pinned-image test jobs run in parallel, and a red one is still a defect to fix before merging (see *Tests*).
- **Scorecard stays off.** The scan passes no `--scs-repo-url`/`--scs-repo-token`, because Checkmarx One cannot reach the IP-allowlisted org. `scs` stays in `--scan-types` for secret detection, so expect one "Unable to start Scorecard scan" warning per run. Do not add `--scs-engines` to `additional_params` to silence it: the warning is the accepted price, and any error in `checkmarx-scan` blocks every merge. Re-enable only once the org is reachable, with the URL built from `github.server_url` + `github.repository`, never a literal (lock: `tests/ci-config-lockstep.spec.js`).
- **The repository is `github.com/cx-bizops/se-hackarena`; every text file must name it by that address,** since an older one works only through GitHub's transfer redirect. Only AGENT_AUDIT_MEMORY.md (the operator's file) is exempt; a subdirectory with its own `.git` (agent worktree, submodule) is skipped, and a planted-tree control proves the walk still reads ordinary directories (lock: `tests/ci-config-lockstep.spec.js`).

## Reference

### Configuration via environment variables

README.md *Environment Variables* is canonical (DEVELOPMENT.md defers to it); update it with the code. Full set: grep `process.env.` in `server.js db.js lib/ routes/`.

#### Rules

- **Five environment checks abort boot:** a missing `BASE_URL` (`lib/config.js`), no mail relay (`SMTP_HOST` unset and none saved in Settings; `lib/email.js`), `DATA_DIR` or `UPLOADS_DIR` (`lib/data-dir.js`); outside `NODE_ENV=test`, an `https://` `BASE_URL` with trust proxy off (see *Boot-time sanity checks*).
- **Test `app.get("trust proxy")`, never the raw `TRUST_PROXY` string:** `"false"` and `"0"` are JS-truthy. `lib/util.js` `parseTrustProxy` is the one reader; its keywords (`true`, `false`, the presets) are case-insensitive, as Express reads any other spelling as an address and stops boot (lock: `tests/trust-proxy-keywords.spec.js`).
- **Any of `NODE_ENV=production`, an `https://` `BASE_URL` or trust proxy on forces Secure cookies** (`SECURE_COOKIE`); plain HTTP needs all three off.
- **Each reader parses its own booleans.** `HSTS` is off only on `0`/`false`/`off`/`no`; `LOG_*` and `SUPERADMIN_PASSWORD_QUIET` take `1`/`true`/`yes`, `RATE_LIMIT_DISABLED` `1`/`true`; `SMTP_ALLOW_PLAINTEXT`, `MINIFY_JS` and `CXONE_WITHDRAWAL_AUTORUN_IN_TEST` need exactly `1`.
- **Read a new multi-module knob once, in `lib/config.js`** (as `PERF_SPAN_SLOW_MS` is), so an override reaches every consumer. Existing exceptions (read in each module): `RATE_LIMIT_DISABLED` (`lib/middleware.js`, `server.js`), `RATE_LIMIT_LOGIN_*` (`server.js`, `routes/auth.js`).
- **Not configurable:** the session secret and the superadmin password, both random every boot (see *Built-in superadmin*).

#### Variables

| Name | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `BASE_URL` | required | Public origin, trailing `/` stripped |
| `PORT` | `3000` | HTTP port |
| `DATA_DIR`, `UPLOADS_DIR` | required | Data and separate uploads volumes; no default on purpose (see *Architecture*) |
| `NODE_ENV` | unset | `production`: JS bundle, no console mirror. `test`: `/api/test/*` routes, background jobs off |
| `SMTP_HOST` | required unless a relay is saved in Settings | SMTP relay; a relay saved in Settings → Email (SMTP) replaces every `SMTP_*`/`EMAIL_FROM` value below but the pool sizes and `SMTP_ALLOW_PLAINTEXT` |
| `SMTP_PORT` | `587` | `465` is implicit TLS; others require STARTTLS |
| `SMTP_USER`, `SMTP_PASS` | unset | Auth sent only if `SMTP_USER` is set |
| `SMTP_ALLOW_PLAINTEXT` | off | `1` drops `requireTLS`; local capture relays only |
| `SMTP_POOL_MAX_CONNECTIONS`, `SMTP_POOL_MAX_MESSAGES` | `16` (1-64), `100` (1-10000) | Pooled relay connections; messages per connection before recycling |
| `EMAIL_FROM` | `no-reply@hackathon.local` | Sender address only, unless a relay is saved in Settings (see *Email and alerts*) |
| `COMPETITION_NAME` | `Checkmarx Enablement Platform` | Deprecated; read only if `settings.platform_name` is empty |
| `TRUST_PROXY` | off | Hop count (preferred), preset, IP/CIDR list, or `true` (trusts every hop: `req.ip` forgeable; boot warns); keywords in any case |
| `HSTS` | on | Strict-Transport-Security header |
| `SUPERADMIN_PASSWORD_QUIET` | off | Hides the stdout password banner |
| `EXTERNAL_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` | `10000` | Outbound CxOne deadline, body included |
| `RATE_LIMIT_DISABLED` | off | All limiters and the scanner tracker off; dev only |
| `RATE_LIMIT_LOGIN_PER_MIN`, `RATE_LIMIT_LOGIN_IP_BACKSTOP_PER_MIN` | `60`, `600` | Per (IP, name) on `/api/login`, `/api/auth/password`, `/api/auth/sign-in-method`; per-IP backstop on those and the code redeem (× 15 on the code request) |
| `RATE_LIMIT_PROOF_IP_PER_15MIN` | `150` | Address proofs per IP per 15 minutes, refused silently (test servers pin 40) |
| `RATE_LIMIT_CSRF_PER_MIN`, `RATE_LIMIT_CONFIG_PER_MIN` | `120`, `120` | `/api/csrf-token`; `/api/config` and `/api/settings/appearance` (separate buckets) per IP |
| `RATE_LIMIT_ALLOWLIST_IPS`; `LOG_SKIP_IPS` | unset | Exact IPs and IPv4 CIDRs (IPv6 CIDRs never match). Bypass limiters and scanner tracker; omit from access log |
| `LOG_DIR`, `LOG_LEVEL` | `DATA_DIR/logs`, `info` | Log streams; minimum app-log level |
| `BACKUP_DIR` | unset | A read-only mount of the host's backups folder; Settings → About & health counts them and dates the newest (`lib/backup-status.js`); unset: "not visible" |
| `BUILD_COMMIT`, `BUILD_TIME`, `RELEASE_CHANNEL` | unset | Baked into the release image (Dockerfile ARG/ENV, `.github/workflows/release-image.yml`); shown under About & health (`lib/build-info.js`); unset or malformed: unknown |
| `LOG_CONSOLE_MIRROR`, `LOG_DISABLE_ACCESS` | on unless production; off | Mirror logs to stdout (off, a non-zero exit still replays recent errors to stderr); skip the access log |
| `LOG_MIN_FREE_MB`, `_PCT` | `500`, `5` | Below this, delete the oldest rotated logs, never today's |
| `LOG_SCAN_BUDGET`, `LOG_SORT_MAX_WINDOW` | `200000` (min 1000), `25000` (min 100) | Lines `GET /api/admin/logs` walks (decides whether totals are exact); deepest offset+limit ranked across matches |
| `PERF_SPAN_SLOW_MS` | `300` | Slow `[perf-span]` threshold |
| `EVENT_RETENTION_DAYS` | `730` (below 1 falls back) | Daily `*_events` purge retention; also the age a CxOne removal is flagged past (never dropped) |
| `LOGIN_CODE_TTL_MS`, `_MAX_SENDS` | `600000` (min 60000), `3` (min 1) | Sign-in code lifetime; sends per window |
| `MINIFY_JS` | on in production | `1` forces the bundle locally; production cannot turn it off |
| `CXONE_WITHDRAWAL_AUTORUN_IN_TEST` | unset | Test harness; read only under `NODE_ENV=test` |

### API routes summary

- **`swagger.yaml` is the API contract; this is only a map.** Update it with any route, field or status change. `tests/swagger-conformance.spec.js` holds its shape, its security claims and that every registered route is documented (and every documented one registered; `/api/test/*` left out by rule, other differences listed with a reason); response fields are held by review.
- **Keep it valid OpenAPI 3.0.3:** quoted status codes, a `description` on every response, no duplicate keys, true per-operation `security` (no GET names `csrfHeader`, the X-CSRF-Token scheme; public routes omit `sessionAuth`). No example or prose line may read as `token:`/`password:` plus a value: secret scanners flag it (SW-7). **It must parse under a strict YAML parser:** quote a scalar holding `: ` (PyYAML and other consumers refuse it, while the suite's line reader accepts it), checked with the `yaml` that `playwright-core/lib/utilsBundle` exports (no new dependency). (lock: `tests/swagger-conformance.spec.js`)
- **Every operation lists the shared refusals it can really produce, as `$ref`s** (`BadRequest`, `TooManyRequests`, `InternalError`, `Unauthorized`, `Forbidden`, `CsrfRefused`, `NotFound`, `EventNotFound`, `UnsupportedMediaType`), never one it cannot to quiet an API-documentation scanner (`/healthz`, `/readyz` run before the parsers and the scanner block; public operations never 401; a parameterless path has no 404 unless its handler has a not-found branch): such findings are triaged false positives. Also in `components/responses`: `QueryParamRepeated` (every `/api/admin/` GET documents it, by `$ref` or named in its own 400; lock: `tests/swagger-conformance.spec.js`), `CompetitionChanged`, `ProfileIncomplete`, and `UnexpectedError`, every operation's `default`.
- **`PUT /api/me` has no `required:` list on purpose**: first-party callers send sparse bodies. **`CompetitorCannotEdit` is deliberately unreferenced**, cited in prose by multi-cause 403s.

#### Route modules

Registration shape: one default-exported `register(app[, deps])` per module (see *Key files*).

| Module | Holds |
|---|---|
| `auth` | register, verify, confirm-email, unsubscribe, sign-in codes, password door, sign-in-method lookup and change, logout |
| `participant` | `/api/me`, status, timeslots, participant task reads, hints |
| `self-enroll`, `badge`, `leaderboard` | join links/codes and leave; badges, `/credential`, Program grades (Try again, the organisers' grades list); boards |
| `competitions`, `sessions`, `challenges` | admin events (incl. clone and event templates), sessions, tasks and attachments |
| `certificate-config` | certificate design: global, per-event override, preview |
| `event-look` | per-event look (`PUT /api/admin/competitions/:id/look`) |
| `event-overview` | workspace Overview's live read (`GET /api/admin/competitions/:id/overview-pulse`) |
| `topics` | the Question bank's topics (list, create, rename, delete with move) |
| `event-team` | event team, targets, persona reports, lead marks, the allowed-domain list |
| `participants`, `groups`, `invitations` | admin users, groups, invitations |
| `users` | Roles & permissions (the page once called Users & roles): admin accounts (list, add), custom roles CRUD (`requirePermission`; role changes use the shared handler in `participants`). `GET /api/admin/roles` says per role whether this session may give it or move an account off it (`can_give`/`can_take`, `roleAssignDenied`); the dialogs offer only those. `role_changed` records custom labels (`from_label`/`to_label`) |
| `audit` | `GET /api/admin/audit`, the Audit page's one stream over the admin `*_events` tables (never `challenge_events`), `task_versions` (metadata only), submissions, hints and `orphan_events`, scoped per source (module header). **It pages like Raw logs:** the first page alone is counted (capped at `AUDIT_COUNT_MAX`) and names its `anchor` (newest row); a jump is that anchor plus `offset` (at most `AUDIT_OFFSET_MAX`), Next and Prev a `cursor`, which also searches on past a walk the budget cut (locks: `tests/audit-page.spec.js`, `tests/audit-pane-controls.spec.js`). **Each kind of row follows the permission that guards it elsewhere** (`SOURCE_PERMISSIONS`; a logged event naming no event needs `audit.raw_logs`; operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026): naming an unreadable kind is 403 `audit_type_forbidden`; `readable_types` drives the chips (lock: `tests/audit-row-permissions.spec.js`). A walk no index leads (`lib/query-plan.js`) reads at most `AUDIT_SCAN_BUDGET` rows per source per read, ending the page at the newest cut (`searched_to`); a person record's event filter rides `idx_user_events_competition`, whose expression a query must repeat (`JX("details", "$.competition_id")`) or it walks by time (lock: `tests/audit-walk-budget.spec.js`) |
| `smtp-settings` | Settings → Email (SMTP): the mail server in use (`GET /api/admin/smtp-settings`), its save, removal and the unsaved-values test |
| `export-import`, `settings`, `events` | exports (incl. full event and person exports, the top-3 certificates ZIP, the join QR renderer); settings (incl. the platform look `PUT /api/admin/platform-look`, public `GET /api/settings/appearance`); audit-history reads |
| `admin-misc` | alerts, retention, default CxOne, withdrawals, test-email, unsubscribed, the caller's own address (Use my current address), `GET /api/admin/system-health` (Settings → Backups & health; `settings.view`: coarse facts from server.js's `healthState` dep, never a path or driver message; database size, Node version and start time only for a superadmin; lock `tests/system-health.api.spec.js`) |

#### Inline in server.js

Why each stays inline (see *Key files*):

- `POST /api/tasks/:id/submit` (perf-span shape is source-pinned); the two `POST /api/admin/import/*`, `GET /api/admin/submissions` and `/api/admin/logs` (source scanners read them there).
- `GET /metrics` and `/api/admin/analytics/challenges`: own or read process-resident singletons.
- `POST /api/login` (built-in superadmin only) mutates in-process superadmin-password state.
- Also probes, `csp-report`, `csrf-token`, `images/:id`, `asset-versions.js`, perf bulk-seed.

#### Non-obvious route facts

- **`/healthz` is liveness, always 200; never make it DB-aware.** `/readyz` is what `HEALTHCHECK`, compose and load balancers probe.
- **Tag delete routes use `req.params.tag` as is**: Express already decoded it.
- **Submissions' default order is a branch-capped walk, valid only for `event_time DESC`.** Other `SUB_SORTS` keys take the materialising path; every order ends `event_time DESC, tie_key DESC`; unknown keys are ignored.
- **Superadmin gating is wide and not listed here.** ROLES_AND_PERMISSIONS.md is the authority; grep `requireSuperAdmin`. Trap: `PATCH /api/admin/settings` is superadmin-only while `GET /api/admin/settings` (and `/settings/events`) is open to any global admin but refused to event admins by `eventAdminGuard` (`EVENT_ADMIN_FORBIDDEN` in `lib/event-admin.js`).

### Accepted audit dispositions (intentional, do not re-flag)

Current decisions (code or operator ruling); change none without an operator decision. `AGENT_AUDIT_MEMORY.md` (the operator's file) records the accepted-by-design external-audit findings, including deployment items not repeated here.

#### Identity and sign-in

- **ASCII-only nickname/email charset.** Makes the NOCASE unique indexes complete; `normaliseEmail` is defence only. `lib/util.js`.
- **Bulk-seed and import skip the corporate-email rule.** `server.js` `bulk-seed-users`.
- **No emailed sign-in link.** A GET redemption page mints an anonymous CSRF session row per view.
- **The sign-in-method answer tells whether an address is a password account** (by default every unscoped admin; operator ruling, 11 Nov 2026). Unknown, code, blocked and denied addresses all answer `code`. `routes/auth.js`.
- **SMS OTP deferred.** SMS cannot carry the first greylisted message; cost and SMS-pumping exposure.
- **Admin-tier sign-in from a join form or join link enrols nothing** (`staffRowRefusal`, `_enrolAtSignIn`; operator ruling, 17 Nov 2026); an admin-tier author of a compete event stays unenrolled.
- **Trusted network IPs are admin-settable.** Bounded by `eventPolicy` `trustedNetworks`, the `GRACE_MS` window, `MIN_CIDR_BITS`, `MAX_ENTRIES`, audit and lockout. `lib/comp-allowlist.js`.
- **The `csrf_invalid` `POST /api/logout` and the one `csrf_invalid` retry in `_sessionTransitionPost` stay** though redundant. Logout POST: `public/js/core/api.js` (lock: `tests/functional-core-a.api.spec.js`); retry (harmless): `public/js/features/impersonation.js`.
- **An admin email edit is a correction:** the old address holds until confirmed; Block acts immediately. **Any non-changing self-save cancels a staged change** (the only cancel control).
- **A recipient deleted before a queued send counts as a failure;** the orphaned `email_sent` keeps `to`. `lib/email.js`.
- **Three sign-in answers differ by one database write** (operator ruling, 24 Nov 2026; not to be equalised): an untie (`POST /api/auth/code/drop-event`) that clears a binding writes its audit row and one that finds nothing does not; a code request for an address nobody holds writes the proof row and one for an existing account does not; past an IP's refusal-record budget a refusal writes nothing, so a caller who fills that budget learns one bit per IP per window. Bodies and cookies are identical; the timing gap is unmeasured and sits behind the per-(IP, address) and per-IP limits. Password-account disclosure is separate (above). `routes/auth.js`.

#### Events, challenges and scoring

- **Reviving an auto-ended event past its end stays superadmin-only** (operator ruling, 25 Nov 2026; `routes/competitions.js`, `routes/sessions.js`; text `routes/challenges.js` `FROZEN_REMEDY`). A regular admin gets only attempt grants.
- **Programs have no default attempt cap;** the penalty shows, guessing is unbounded. `attempt_cap` exists.
- **`ACTIVE_PARTICIPANT_SQL` has no profile-completeness term,** so incomplete profiles suppress all-solved auto-end. Safe side; ends on schedule. `lib/competitions.js`.
- **`any` challenges solve on the first non-empty answer;** `is_correct` consumers stay type-blind.
- **`reveal_answers_on_end` on a Booth Event shows answers to late entrants.** The form warns; do not suppress.
