- **No self-enrolment ceiling.**
- **The self-enroll token preview keeps its uniform 404** (see *Competition lifecycle*).
- **Scores may go negative.**
- **Participant leaderboard has no search box** (would leak hidden rows; lock: `tests/longlist-456.spec.js`) **and exposes opaque `userId`s** (row diffing).
- **Brief staleness accepted:** leaderboard `solveTimeMs`, `/api/me` score after recalculate, no timeslot cache (see *In-process caches and their invalidation*).
- **Impersonated actions count in submissions, hints and leaderboard score;** only analytics excludes them; impersonated requests bank no presence and stamp no `login_time` (see *Scoring*). `navigated-away` stays allowed under read-only impersonation.
- **Near-miss tag guard is on the typed write only;** restore and import accept near misses.
- **The answer seal is not encryption at rest;** backups copy `correct_answer` by construction.
- **The tasks export gives a competitor an unsealed key the admin list withholds.** No door reaches that state (staff-compete events admit sealed challenges only); never null the key: that makes the row unimportable. `routes/export-import.js`.

#### Certificates, privacy and data

- **A deleted person's address and nickname stay in the record of their deletion** (`deleted`, the reaper's `account_reaped`: written after the cascade into `orphan_events` and app.jsonl, purged after `EVENT_RETENTION_DAYS`). Operator ruling, 15 Nov 2026: kept and documented (README erasure step 3 gives the SQL; lock: `tests/person-delete-erasure.api.spec.js`, which also holds that certificate mail, share links and lead marks go with the person).

- **`/badge/:token` is crawlable; no consent copy at registration.** Newer pages publish only on Share (see *Achievement badges and certificates*).
- **`/credential` shows the current name and discloses a change;** name-fallback certificates print the nickname; diacritic-only changes go unreported. `routes/badge.js`.
- **Admins may mint, not publish, while impersonating.** Sole carve-out in `impersonationReadOnlyWriteAllowed`; do not move it. `lib/impersonation.js`.
- **No notice that enrolment sends data to Checkmarx One.** If added: notice plus link, never a consent checkbox.
- **`competitions.cxone_disabled` has no normalising migration for Programs** (operator ruling, 25 Nov 2026: no action needed, rows not counted): a Program created before 8.2.0 may hold a 0 an admin never chose and provision against its kind's default, and a 0 chosen then cannot be told from a forced one. Booth rows were normalised once (`db.js` marker `booth_cxone_off_at`, each recorded as an `edited` event by `system`), as a Booth Event never creates accounts (owner's decision, 27 Nov 2026).
- **CxOne refuses a pre-created user carrying the secondary address.** Indistinguishable from takeover. `lib/cxone.js`.
- **Client-side CSV exports emit no `data_exported`;** egress was the data GET.
- **Pre-fingerprint invitation ids in `app-*.jsonl` stay until `scripts/purge.sh` deletes those rotated files.**
- **The Logs mirror is eventually consistent** (see *Audit trail*); a lost post-commit `auto_ended` is reconstructable from `ended_at`.

#### Operations and UI

- **`package.json` `overrides` are security pins, each with a reason:** `debug ^4` (compression and express-session pin 2.6.9, a ReDoS; 4.x ships no `Makefile`, so `debug` is not in the prune table), `qs ^6.17.1` (express and body-parser still accept the vulnerable 6.16.0/6.16.1), nodemon's `chokidar ^4` (drops `braces`, `anymatch` and `picomatch` 2, with no fixed release; nodemon still filters its `ignore` globs itself: a `docs/screenshots/**` write restarts nothing, measured) and nodemon's `minimatch ^11.0.3` (GHSA-g3hv-75wg-m249, a dev dependency). Delete an override once its parent ships the fix. The lockfile SBOM lists the pruned `node-addon-api`.
- **Container findings on the two base images are triaged, not fixed:** `node:26-alpine`'s are npm's bundled dependencies, deleted by the runtime stage; `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright`'s are a test-only image's OS packages with no newer base (it moves in lockstep with `@playwright/test`). The CI Dockerfile has no `USER` on purpose (the job containers need root; see its last comment).
- **GHSA-2v37-7h3g-55p8 (nanoid) is unreachable:** only postcss pulls it in, via `nanoid/non-secure`. The lockfile still holds the fixed floor (lock: `tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js`; fails if the entry vanishes).
- **A stale base image is not a production issue:** the redeploy builds with `--pull --no-cache`; compose sets `build.pull: true`.
- **Node `requestTimeout` stays default (300 s);** shorter cuts venue uploads. Safe only behind a body-buffering proxy (nginx's default `proxy_request_buffering on`): it closes the client-held part of the in-flight-revocation window, which a streaming proxy (Caddy, Traefik, ALB) or an exposed app port reopens.
- **An empty `.maintenance` file is maintenance with no bypass for anyone, operators included, until a token is written; only removing the file ends it.** Fail-closed; use the README recipe.
- **`npm start`/`dev` keep `${DATA_DIR:-./data}`;** only operator scripts refuse.
- **No OSSF Scorecard;** the org is IP-allowlisted (see *Deployment and CI image*).
- **The access log hooks only `res.on("finish")`;** an abandoned upload leaves one `app.jsonl` warn line (`Request abandoned`, status 499, full context) with no access line; a multer limit refusal still answers 413. `server.js`.
- **Pagination `parseInt` stays lenient;** clamped, never stored.
- **The participant import holds the event loop while it checks and writes** (1-2 s for 5 000 rows): yielding between chunks is not wanted (operator rulings, 24 and 25 Nov 2026), as it would make every up-front conflict decision stale.
- **WebKit `<select>` is taller (no `appearance`); `border-radius:6px` and `#comp-internal` are grandfathered.**

### Known open gaps

Known, not yet fixed and not accepted. Listed so an audit does not file them as new; re-file one if it has grown or a fix has become cheap, and never assume one covered. Accepted behaviour is in *Accepted audit dispositions*.

#### Operator actions outstanding

- **Delete the `REPO_TOKEN` secret and revoke its PAT at the owner.** Deleting the secret does not kill a token past scan configurations hold.

#### Server and data

- The image sweep's week counts from when a picture was STORED, not from when its last user went (`lib/image-sweep.js` selects by `created_at`), so an old picture whose last challenge is deleted goes at the next pass, and a replaced avatar is reclaimed at once while open screens and 5 s caches may still name it.
- The orphaned-image sweep's reference read (`lib/image-sweep.js` `collectReferencedImageIds`) holds the event loop for its whole length, which grows with stored text (`task_versions` gains a snapshot at every challenge save and is never purged); it is unmeasured at scale, only reported per pass (`images_refs_ms`).
- A tag or role change landing inside one automatic certificate run reaches the rest of the run only through the person's own `off_board` refusal (which resets the job's board memo): issues already past their eligibility read, and people ranked below someone already holding a certificate when tagged, keep the rank the memo gave them until a timed retry rebuilds it (`lib/certificate-dispatch.js`).
- A chunked multipart body, a multer-refused one (read to its end) and parts busboy skips are bounded in length only by `requestTimeout` (bandwidth, not memory); the submit route's declared-length gate sees only a Content-Length.
- **A deleted person's nickname and userId stay on every frozen team board** (`group_leaderboard_snapshots` solo rows, served to the event's participants by `GET /api/leaderboard`): the deletion strikes only the avatar (`lib/account-reaper.js` `_scrubBoardAvatars`). Whether to replace them too awaits an operator ruling; README's erasure procedure (step 3) says so.
- A pre-body refusal of a body over `REFUSE_DRAIN_MAX_BYTES` is cut, which Firefox and WebKit show as a network error.
- A Devanagari (Hindi) name prints the nickname: resvg cannot shape it (operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026: keep the nickname; revisit at the next dependency update, where a newer renderer may). An event name, or the platform name a design's `{platform}` fills (the default hub line too, and a rename after a design was saved), mixing Latin into Arabic or in a script no face draws can print `.notdef` boxes: neither has a fallback. Certificate wording itself is refused at save, except a layer stored before the check, which keeps rendering until its next save.
- Recalculate under `any` still credits two kinds of older multiple-choice row that picked no option: one naming only ids that were never options (stored before submit refused it) and one predating `answer_canonical`. Once the challenge leaves multiple choice its options are gone, so neither can be told from a real pick.
- Retention purges `challenge_events` but not `submissions`/`hint_reveals` (`lib/event-retention.js` `EVENT_TABLES`); past the window, the export's Navigated Away reads 0.
- A large challenge import still holds the event loop while its transaction writes (about 3 s for 10 000 rows).
- `lib/login-code.js` keeps its own `ALPHABET`/`normaliseCode`, folding U to V where `lib/human-code.js` rejects U: the recorded exemption until the U case is decided.
- **Secondary-address squatting** (claiming another's address as your secondary) reserves it, so its owner cannot register it; it signs nobody in, and invitations and "Add user" refuse it (see *Sign-in codes*), but the superadmin participant import still matches a conflict on either address. Duplicate people (two accounts, two addresses) are not merged.
- Perf tooling signs in with emailed codes read back from a Mailpit relay (`perf/k6/lib/mailbox.js`, `MAILPIT_URL`); `perf/run-local.sh`'s own SMTP sink has no inbox API, so its scenarios need a Mailpit on its SMTP port (perf/README.md § "1000-user sign-in storm").

#### Admin flows

- **Event-owned questions (stage A) leave three things undecided:** the challenge import's overwrite reaches only library (bank) rows, never an event's same-titled copy; platform-wide analytics lists each event's copy as a row of its own (no grouping by origin); and the competitor lock follows LINEAGE (`competitorEventsForTask`: a question, its bank question and every copy), which refuses a competitor more than the event they compete in strictly needs.
- **An existing waiting account invited by a role that cannot approve** is skipped and reported in that invitation's result (`skippedUnapproved`), and nothing records the invitation afterwards: its organiser is not shown it waiting.
- **The competition save is not one transaction.** Session bulk-delete commits before the PUT (a refused PUT leaves them gone; the toast appends `_seam`), `validate_only` misses in-transaction refusals, and the dry-run tag sync after it is best-effort. The fix is one save endpoint shaped like the form.
- Not re-checked at commit: admin reads that disclose after an await (`GET /api/admin/export/tasks`, `GET /api/admin/logs`, the single admin certificate PDF and image downloads; the top-three ZIP and the full event and person exports do, through `{read:true}`), and an event admin's scope at the badge mint (only the hand's lifecycle and tier).
- Sealed reveal: past `busy_timeout` the explicit reveal POST answers a generic 500 (fail-closed); the hint POST records outside the INSERT's transaction, so a failed INSERT over-records. `impersonation_started`, `login_code_revealed` and `credential_resolved` swallow a failed audit write.
- Late entry: entrants get no notice; sent emails keep the old deadline; a stored invitation redeemed by a new account still joins a closed event; absence rows need an entry (`login_time` or `last_login_time`).
- Late entry: group unbind, delete and sync REMOVE keep non-late members and late entrants holding a placing certificate. A superadmin re-pin can reopen play and a stale Close confirm undo it (last act wins; the audit's `from` shows what it replaced). Regenerate is offered to a regular admin on an ended event (open product question).
- `lib/staff-compete.js` `_competitorRemedy` tells an out-of-scope competing event admin to leave (only the API can) and a competing late entrant to ask another organiser (they could leave).
- The share card (`buildBadgeSvg`) is not configurable. Templates (named saved designs) do not exist; a design is the global layer plus one override per event.
- An older challenge tagged `qualifying:` that is not a pick-one question (the tag routes once allowed it), or whose key is malformed (an import stored one before refusing it), keeps the tag: it scores nothing, and no report reads its answers until it is made a valid pick-one question or loses the tag.
- The fixed-options rule (`qualifying_options_fixed`) binds only a TAGGED question: removing its `qualifying:` tag, reordering its options and re-adding the tag re-classifies the answers already given (accepted, operator ruling, 14 Nov 2026; an ended session freezes both tag routes, `qualifying_tag_frozen`).

#### Client

- **A timer-fired `showMain`/`showPage` over the list or a challenge bumps `S._taskViewGen` and drops an in-flight open.** Only a comment in `public/js/ui/nav.js` guards this; keep new timers off this path.
- A select-competition applied AFTER the deselect the tab sent on leaving for the list (Events or Back) re-pins the event server-side (`active_competition_id`, `left_event_at` NULL), undoing that opt-out for the next sign-in, though the tab stands down and stays on the list. Any pick then in flight (the pending page's poll, a card pick, the admin-view toggle's re-select, a join) can do it if the two requests arrive reordered.
- The cross-tab token check (see *Sessions and CSRF*) misses a write another tab sends between this tab's session replacement and its token fetch, and a browser without storage (where a sign-out in another tab also still reads as inactivity); each still meets `csrf_invalid`, which ends the live session.
- Tap view (`public/js/features/choice-tap.js`): View as Participant shows the standard card; "Not correct" lasts one page view; no per-event opt-out; an over-long question scrolls rather than shrinking past `s2`.
- `#invite-group-filter` (`features/participants-admin.js`) obeys the sibling rule only because nothing re-renders its box.

#### CxOne

- CxOne withdrawal and takeover residuals (see *CxOne (Checkmarx One) provisioning*).

## UI Polish Protocol

`/polish <target>` (slug, csv, `all` or file glob) runs `.claude/commands/polish.md`.

### Source of truth

- **`.claude/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` owns every UI token; the aesthetic is Flat Design 3.0 with two flat looks, Signature and Ambient** (operator ruling, 13 Nov 2026: construction is Flat Design 3.0's, a look changes colour and corner radii only; certificate grammar; one gold action per screen). The `frontend-design` skill applies it and never replaces it.
- **Hex is authoritative and there are no `oklch()` declarations**; the palette is generated (see *CSS custom properties*). A palette change needs a visual-baseline regeneration in the same commit, because it shifts every screen (see *Tests*).

### Subagent routing

- **`ui-polisher` handles one screen per call and edits only the grep-scoped regions of the files its manifest entry names** (markup, styles, behaviour plus `behaviour.also`), plus a minimal registration edit in `core/main.js` `boot()`. It never edits outside `public/`, even when a slot points there (`routes/auth.js`), and keeps ids, `data-*`, handlers, ARIA, `overlayDismiss` and focus traps.
- **At most 5 polishers run in parallel, and batches run in sequence**, because every screen shares `styles.css`. `ui-visual-verifier` always runs alone.

### Visual verification

- **Screenshots come only from `POLISH_PHASE=<before|after> POLISH_SCREENS=<csv|all> POLISH_MATRIX=<modes|full|looks|house> npm run polish:shots`**, writing gitignored `.claude/visual-diffs/<screen>@<look>-<mode>-<width>-<phase>.png` (`house`: `<screen>-<phase>.png`); the default `modes` is the house look by day and night at 1280 and 390 px. Never use the Playwright MCP, another Playwright install or a direct `npx playwright test` for them (VERIFY's axe run is the one sanctioned direct invocation).
- **A route step that throws, an unrecognised step verb, or a `visual_anchor` that never becomes visible fails the capture**, since it would screenshot the wrong screen. A screen that cannot be captured at all carries `status_note: "uncapturable"` with a reason and is SKIPPED by name; every other screen is reachable, and state the seed does not provide comes from named fixtures (`tests/polish-fixtures/`, step `fixture <name>`). Step language, fixtures and matrices: `tests/_ui-polish.spec.js` (its header and above `runRouteScript`).

### Manifest shape (`.claude/screens.json`)

- Each `screens[]` entry has `screen`, `status`, `route`, `visual_anchor`, `auth`, `route_script` and `files.{markup,behaviour,styles}`, plus optional `looks`/`modes`/`widths` arrays narrowing its matrix (a phone-only sheet: `"widths": [390]`). A slot is `{path, grep, also?}`, or null/omitted when it does not apply; `grep: null` means the whole file.
- **Lock (`tests/functional-core-b.api.spec.js`): paths exist, every `#id`/`data-action` is in app source, every `grep` compiles and matches its own file, and every `public/` file a slot names is inside the ui-polisher's scope grant (`.claude/agents/ui-polisher.md`; a file outside `public/` is read-only context).**
- **Every dialog the SPA mounts has an entry** (lock: "every dialog the SPA mounts…" in the same spec): each `overlayDismiss(` call lies in an entry's scope (the entry names the file and its grep matches the enclosing function up to the call; a null grep is the whole file), and each literal id passed to a dialog factory (a function taking the overlay's `id` first, such as users-admin.js `_dialog`) is named `#id` by an entry. A new dialog gets an entry with a route that opens it, or the owning entry's grep widens to reach it. Pages, panes and popovers are not held.
- **Reach admin sections with `tab <slug>` (`button.admin-nav-item[data-tab-id="admin-<slug>-tab"]`, opening the phone drawer when the sidebar is hidden) and an open workspace's sections with `section <data-section>`, never by label**, because labels are copy and change (the manifest lock refuses label selectors).
- **A slot whose region is built in JS is `null`, never an invented grep**, which would point the polisher at someone else's code.

### The `/polish` workflow

- The flow: verifier *before* (stop on failure), then polisher batches, each followed by an esbuild bundle of `public/js/core/main.js` (`node --check` misses browser-fatal errors), then verifier *after*, then VERIFY, then the report.
- **VERIFY cannot be bypassed.** It runs the split ui-admin/ui-participant axe specs by glob (a named spec that was later split would silently disable the gate), then the design locks whole (`layout-integrity`, `dark-theme-a11y`, `interactive-contrast`, `theme-tokens`, `type-scale-scanner`, `radius-tokens`, `label-in-name`, `help-tips-coverage`, `hover-pulse-no-fade`, `code-blocks`, `signin-fit`, `app-chrome`) and `node scripts/build-theme-tokens.js --check`. Chromium only (not Safari or Firefox).
- The command never commits.

### Do-not touch

`.claude/settings.local.json`, `playwright.config.js` and every existing file under `tests/` (`_ui-polish.spec.js` may be maintained, never during a run). Also anything outside `public/` and the `frontend-design` skill.
